Mr. Speaker, may we acknowledge

**[00:41:55.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000495)  
the presence of the heads

**[00:41:57.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000496)

**[00:41:59.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000497)

**[00:42:01.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000498)  
Secretary Oscar F. Villanueva,

**[00:42:03.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000499)  
Chairperson,

**[00:42:05.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000500)  
Usec Earl P.

**[00:42:07.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000501)  
Saavedra,

**[00:42:09.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000502)  
Asec Maria Victoria

**[00:42:11.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000503)  
C. Sorne,

**[00:42:13.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000504)  
and Asec Ferdowsi Masnar.

**[00:42:15.940] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000505)  
We acknowledge the presence

**[00:42:17.940] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000506)  
of the heads and officials of the Department

**[00:42:19.940] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000507)  
of the Dangerous Drugs Board,

**[00:42:21.940] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000508)  
headed by Chairperson

**[00:42:23.940] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000509)  
Oscar Villanueva.

**[00:42:25.940] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000510)  
Welcome to the House of Representatives.

**[00:42:27.940] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000511)  
Majority Leader.

**[00:42:29.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000512)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to

**[00:42:31.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000513)  
recognize Representative

**[00:42:33.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000514)  
Marcelino Marci R. Teodoro,

**[00:42:35.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000515)  
Vice Chairperson of the Committee

**[00:42:37.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000516)  
on Appropriations, to answer

**[00:42:39.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000517)  
questions on the proposed budget

**[00:42:41.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000518)  
of the Department of Dangerous

**[00:42:43.940] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000519)  
Drugs Board.

**[00:42:44.750] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000519-split-2)  
Representative

**[00:42:45.940] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000520)  
Teodoro is recognized. Please proceed.

**[00:42:47.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000521)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[00:42:49.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000522)  
my distinguished colleagues, a serious

**[00:42:51.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000523)  
anti-drug policy

**[00:42:53.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000524)  
cannot end with enforcement.

**[00:42:55.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000525)  
It must also ask what happens

**[00:42:57.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000526)  
before addiction takes hold,

**[00:42:59.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000527)  
what happens when a person

**[00:43:01.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000528)  
needs treatment, and what happens

**[00:43:03.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000529)  
after rehabilitation when that

**[00:43:05.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000530)  
person tries to return to family

**[00:43:07.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000531)  
and community.

**[00:43:09.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000532)  
That is where the Dangerous Drugs

**[00:43:11.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000533)  
Board has an important role.

**[00:43:13.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000534)  
For many Filipino families,

**[00:43:15.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000535)  
this side of the drug problem is

**[00:43:17.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000536)  
very real. May magulang

**[00:43:19.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000537)  
na hindi naghahanap

**[00:43:21.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000538)  
ng huhulihin. Ang hinahanap

**[00:43:23.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000539)  
niya ay tulong para sa anak niya.

**[00:43:25.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000540)  
The drug problem

**[00:43:27.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000541)  
is not only about stopping

**[00:43:29.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000542)  
supply. It is also about

**[00:43:31.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000543)  
reducing demand,

**[00:43:33.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000544)  
preventing abuse, providing

**[00:43:35.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000545)  
treatment, and helping people

**[00:43:37.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000546)  
recover. DDB

**[00:43:39.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000547)  
is not primarily an arresting

**[00:43:41.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000548)  
agency, and it does

**[00:43:43.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000549)  
not operate the entire treatment

**[00:43:45.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000550)  
and rehabilitation system.

**[00:43:47.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000551)  
Its role is to help government

**[00:43:49.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000552)  
build and coordinate one

**[00:43:51.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000553)  
national strategy,

**[00:43:53.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000554)  
linking enforcement,

**[00:43:55.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000555)  
prevention, treatment,

**[00:43:57.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000556)  
rehabilitation,

**[00:43:59.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000557)  
and reintegration.

**[00:44:03.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000558)  
That may not always be the most

**[00:44:05.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000559)  
visible part of the government's

**[00:44:07.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000560)  
anti-drug effort, but

**[00:44:09.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000561)  
it is essential.

**[00:44:11.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000562)  
Because if enforcement is

**[00:44:13.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000563)  
working in one direction,

**[00:44:15.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000564)  
treatment in another,

**[00:44:17.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000565)  
and local programs somewhere else,

**[00:44:19.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000566)  
then we do not yet have

**[00:44:21.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000567)  
a national strategy.

**[00:44:23.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000568)  
We simply have

**[00:44:25.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000569)  
separate programs.

**[00:44:27.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000570)  
DDB exists to help bring those

**[00:44:29.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000571)  
parts together. The

**[00:44:31.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000572)  
available data show progress,

**[00:44:33.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000573)  
and they also show why sustained

**[00:44:35.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000574)  
policy coordination still

**[00:44:37.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000575)  
matters. The

**[00:44:39.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000576)  
2023 National Household

**[00:44:41.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000577)  
Survey reported

**[00:44:43.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000578)  
that current drug use prevalence

**[00:44:45.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000579)  
declined from

**[00:44:47.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000580)  
2.05% in

**[00:44:49.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000581)  
2019 to

**[00:44:51.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000582)  
1.71%

**[00:44:53.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000583)  
in 2023.

**[00:44:55.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000584)  
That represents approximately

**[00:44:57.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000585)  
1.4 million

**[00:44:59.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000586)  
people in the country.

**[00:45:03.160] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000599)  
That decline is encouraging. Pero hindi ibig sabihin na pwede na tayong maging kampante.

**[00:45:11.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000600)  
Mahigit isang milyong Pilipino pa rin yan.

**[00:45:14.540] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000601)  
And not every person in that number needs the same intervention.

**[00:45:19.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000602)  
Some may require residential treatment.

**[00:45:22.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000603)  
Some may need outpatient services.

**[00:45:27.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000604)  
Others may be better served through community-based rehabilitation.

**[00:45:33.140] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000605)  
May iba na ang kailangan ay continuing support para hindi bumalik sa dating sitwasyon.

**[00:45:39.320] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000606)  
So the task is broader than simply counting facilities.

**[00:45:43.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000607)  
The value of DDB's coordinating role is to help government understand what kind of help is needed,

**[00:45:51.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000608)  
where it is needed, and how people can actually reach it.

**[00:45:57.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000609)  
For Fiscal Year 2027, the Dangerous Drugs Board has a proposed total agency budget of P526.54 million.

**[00:46:09.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000610)  
In Fiscal Year 2026, its total agency budget was P598.17 million.

**[00:46:20.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000611)  
That is a reduction of P71.62 million, or approximately 12%.

**[00:46:27.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000612)  
Its new general appropriations amount to P418.14 million.

**[00:46:34.340] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000613)  
So this is not an agency asking for a large expansion.

**[00:46:39.820] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000614)  
Sa totoo lang, mas maliit pa ang kabuang budget na hinihingi para sa susunod na taon.

**[00:46:45.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000615)  
But DDB is still being asked to coordinate a broad national strategy.

**[00:46:53.480] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000616)  
That is why the proposed amount is reasonable.

**[00:46:57.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000617)  
Within the Fiscal Year 2027 proposal, P169.18 million is allocated to the Drug Abuse Prevention and Control Program.

**[00:47:10.860] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000618)  
Of this amount, P111.69 million is for policy formulation, surveys, research, and studies.

**[00:47:21.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000619)  
Another P32.84 million is for advocacy and information programs.

**[00:47:27.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000620)  
And P24.64 million is for capacity building.

**[00:47:33.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000621)  
The size of the allocation for research and policy deserves context.

**[00:47:38.380] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000622)  
This spending is justified when it gives government a clearer basis for where and how to intervene.

**[00:47:46.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000623)  
Simple lang ang punto.

**[00:47:48.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000624)  
Kung hindi natin alam kung nasaan ang problema, paano natin malalaman kung saan ilalagay ang pera.

**[00:47:55.680] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000625)  
Kung hindi natin alam kung aling programa ang gumagana, baka taon-taon lang tayong gumagastos dahil nakasanayan na.

**[00:48:05.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000626)  
At kung hindi natin alam kung ano ang nangyayari pagkatapos ng treatment, paano natin malalaman kung talagang nagkaroon ng recovery.

**[00:48:17.500] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000627)  
That is why data matters.

**[00:48:19.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000628)  
Because research has a value, or research has value when it guides actual decisions to guide us for future allocation of resources.

**[00:48:29.420] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000629)  
It should help identify where more treatment is needed.

**[00:48:33.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000630)  
It should show where community-based programs should be expanded.

**[00:48:37.660] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000631)  
It should guide where prevention should be strengthened.

**[00:48:40.640] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000632)  
And it should help Congress and the executive identify which interventions are producing the best results.

**[00:48:49.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000633)  
That is where DDB adds value as a policy and coordinating institution.

**[00:48:57.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000634)  
This becomes even more important when we talk about rehabilitation.

**[00:49:02.160] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000635)  
The Department of Health is in the best position to tell Congress the actual treatment capacity of the country.

**[00:49:09.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000636)  
Available beds, occupancy, waiting lists, outpatient services, personnel, equipment,

**[00:49:19.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000637)  
and facility requirements.

**[00:49:22.260] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000638)  
DDB should help bring all of that information together under one national strategy.

**[00:49:30.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000639)  
Kasi maraming ahensya ang gumagalaw.

**[00:49:33.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000640)  
PIDEA handles enforcement.

**[00:49:36.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000641)  
DOH handles much of the health and treatment side.

**[00:49:41.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000642)  
LGUs handle many community-based programs.

**[00:49:45.600] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000643)  
Other agencies help with social support.

**[00:49:48.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000644)  
And reintegration.

**[00:49:50.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000645)  
Kaya mahalaga na may isang malinaw na direksyon at isang national strategy.

**[00:49:58.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000646)  
Maraming ahensya ang may programa.

**[00:50:01.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000647)  
Maraming ahensya rin ang may pondo.

**[00:50:04.380] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000648)  
Ang tungkulin ng DDB ay tulungang pagdugtungin ang mga ito.

**[00:50:08.780] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000649)  
Para sa dulo, malinaw kung paano nagkakaugnay-ugnay ang mga programa

**[00:50:13.660] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000650)  
at kung paano nito matutulungan o natutulungan ang Pilipinong nangayon.

**[00:50:18.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000651)  
At kung paano nito nangangailangan ng servisyo.

**[00:50:22.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000652)  
That is the value of a strong coordinating institution.

**[00:50:27.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000653)  
And one reason DDB's capacity deserves continued support.

**[00:50:33.020] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000654)  
The NEP also recognizes that rehabilitation funding extends beyond DDB's regular appropriation

**[00:50:39.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000655)  
with statutory funding sources involving PCSO and Pagco revenues for rehabilitation activities

**[00:50:47.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000656)  
and government services.

**[00:50:48.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000657)  
The purpose of these funding streams is to translate appropriations into services that people can actually reach.

**[00:51:00.660] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000658)  
Doon dapat makita ang halaga ng pondo kung maaabot ang servisyo.

**[00:51:06.620] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000659)  
Kung may nanay sa probinsya na gustong ipagamot ang anak niya, dapat may malinaw siyang mapupuntahan.

**[00:51:14.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000660)  
Kung natapos ang rehab, dapat may support sa pagbalik sa komunidad.

**[00:51:18.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000661)  
At kung may community-based program, alam natin dapat kung alin ang talagang efektibo.

**[00:51:27.020] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000662)  
Those are the outcomes this budget should help government deliver.

**[00:51:32.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000663)  
Mr. Speaker, a credible anti-drug policy has to know the difference between those who profit from illegal drugs

**[00:51:40.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000664)  
and those who are trying to free themselves from dependence.

**[00:51:44.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000665)  
The distinction should remain.

**[00:51:47.620] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000666)  
Sa sindikato, matatagal.

**[00:51:48.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000667)  
Sa tagang batas.

**[00:51:50.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000668)  
Sa taong gustong magbago, may tulong at may daan pabalik.

**[00:51:55.680] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000669)  
And between those two responsibilities, enforcement on one side, recovery on the other,

**[00:52:04.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000670)  
we need policy that connects the work of government.

**[00:52:08.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000671)  
That is where DDB matters.

**[00:52:10.800] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000672)  
So the purpose of this budget is not simply to maintain an office.

**[00:52:14.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000673)  
It is to maintain the institution that helps government.

**[00:52:17.580] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000674)  
See them.

**[00:52:18.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000675)  
To prevent our problem and respond to it as one.

**[00:52:21.580] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000676)  
And one clear objective.

**[00:52:23.580] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000677)  
Fewer Filipino harmed by illegal drugs and more Filipinos given a real chance to recover.

**[00:52:31.680] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000678)  
Yan ang mahalagang trabaho ng DDB.

**[00:52:34.680] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000679)  
And for these reasons, I rise to sponsor and respectfully urge this House to approve the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Dangerous Drugs Board.

**[00:52:46.140] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000680)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:52:48.760] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000681)  
Thank you very much.

**[00:52:50.360] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000682)  
Majority Leader.

**[00:52:51.720] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000683)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to recognize Representative Presley C. De Jesus of Partylist, Filireka,

**[00:53:00.620] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-000684)  
to interpolate the sponsor on the proposed budget of the Dangerous Drugs Board.

**[00:53:06.060] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-000685)  
Representative Presley De Jesus is recognized to interpolate the sponsor.

**[00:53:10.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000686)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:53:12.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000687)  
May the good sponsor yield some questions from this representation.

**[00:53:18.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000688)  
May first question, para alam po ng ating mga kababayan na nakikinig.

**[00:53:24.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000689)  
What is the primary function of DDB or Dangerous Drugs Board?

**[00:53:32.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000690)  
Basically, DDB is a coordinating agency involved in policy formulation.

**[00:53:40.200] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000691)  
In order for us to adopt a national strategy,

**[00:53:48.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000692)  
to have the national strategy against illegal drugs.

**[00:53:54.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000693)  
Ito ang nagpapatupad ng Philippine Anti-Illegal Drugs Strategy natin.

**[00:54:02.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000694)  
Mr. Speaker, this is my sponsor.

**[00:54:04.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000695)  
Does it include our local government units?

**[00:54:08.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000696)  
Sa community-based rehabilitation program ay kasama ang mga local government unit natin.

**[00:54:16.800] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000697)  
At tulad ng ating nabanggit,

**[00:54:18.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000698)  
ang ibang revenue stream na nakukuha ng DDB,

**[00:54:25.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000699)  
lalo na yung mga itinakda ng batas,

**[00:54:29.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000700)  
ang mga ahensya upang magbigay ng tulong tulad ng PagCorp or PCSO.

**[00:54:38.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000701)  
Ito ang ginagamit ng mga pondo o pantulong para sa mga pagbuo o pagtatatag

**[00:54:46.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000702)  
ng mga community-based rehabilitation.

**[00:54:48.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000703)  
Ito ang ginagamit ng mga rehab treatment na pinamahalaan ng ating mga local government unit.

**[00:54:59.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000704)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:55:00.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000705)  
This is my sponsor.

**[00:55:02.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000706)  
Pwede nyo ba sabihin sa amin ilan ho ba ang member ng board and what's the composition of those members of the DDB?

**[00:55:10.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000707)  
Sino mo bang bumubuo rito?

**[00:55:12.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000708)  
Thank you.

**[00:55:16.640] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000709)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[00:55:18.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000710)  
we have 12 line departments.

**[00:55:22.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000711)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:55:39.840] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000712)  
We have line agencies, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:55:49.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000713)  
We have 12 here identified.

**[00:55:53.140] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000714)  
Coming from first on the enforcement side,

**[00:55:58.380] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000715)  
for example, the Philippine National Police,

**[00:56:02.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000716)  
the PIDEA, NBI, DILG,

**[00:56:07.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000717)  
for prosecution, we have the DOJ,

**[00:56:14.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000718)  
for rehabilitation and reintegration,

**[00:56:18.160] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000719)  
napakahalaga po nito.

**[00:56:20.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000720)  
Meron po tayong CHED kasama dito,

**[00:56:23.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000721)  
DepEd kasama rin,

**[00:56:25.320] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000722)  
ang DSWD para sa mga tulong na kailangan nilang matanggap,

**[00:56:32.860] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000723)  
ang DOLI sa reintegration,

**[00:56:35.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000724)  
lalo na yung katapos na ng mga,

**[00:56:37.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000725)  
yung mga treatment.

**[00:56:40.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000726)  
Yung may mga international commitment din po tayo

**[00:56:46.140] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000727)  
with regards to our anti-illegal drug program,

**[00:56:51.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000728)  
kaya po pati ang DFA ay kasama rin po rito.

**[00:56:57.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000729)

**[00:57:14.980] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000734)  
Ibat-ibang,

**[00:57:16.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000735)

**[00:57:18.740] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000736)

**[00:57:19.840] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000737)

**[00:57:25.480] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000738)

**[00:57:46.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000747)

Thank you, Mr. Speaker,

**[00:57:51.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000749)  
the secret sponsor.

aminado po tayo,

ang pangulo po nito.

**[00:59:09.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000782)

Thank you,

Mr. Speaker,

distinguished sponsor.

Ako yung natutuwa,

at least for check and balance purposes.

But the composition itself,

**[00:59:36.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000796)  
Mr. Speaker.

Meron din pong nagtatanong,

**[01:00:10.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000809-split-2)  
In Section 87 of Republic Act No. 9165, the Special Account in the General Fund, or SAGF, supports government treatment and rehabilitation facilities. How much has the DDB collected and obligated from the SAGF in fiscal year 2026?

**[01:00:33.500] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000810)  
Nung 2026 po, Mr. Speaker, ay meron pong P175 million na nakuha po.

**[01:00:42.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000811)  
P175 million po.

**[01:00:50.260] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000812)  
Ang budget po kasi ng DDB ay maliit lang po, hindi po katulad ng ibang ahensya po na tinatalakay po natin ngayon.

**[01:00:58.660] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000813)  
Ang P60 million.

**[01:01:00.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000814)  
Ang P60 million po ay nanggaling po ito sa Pagcor.

**[01:01:03.680] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000815)  
Ito po yung buwan-buwan ay meron statutorily nilalaan na 5 million.

**[01:01:11.200] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000816)  
At ito naman po yung pinamamahagi sa mga community-based rehab program natin, yung mga treatment facilities na pinamamahalaan ng mga LGU.

**[01:01:24.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000817)  
Isang halimbawa po, kanina nakita ko po si Congressman Dumogan dito.

**[01:01:29.580] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000818)  
Ang bagyo.

**[01:01:30.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000819)  
Ang bagyo po ay meron pong gano'ng allocation po.

**[01:01:35.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000820)  
Ang PCS po ay nakapagbigay po ng 81 million po.

**[01:01:40.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000821)  
Ang MM Metro Manilo Terp Corporation ay 3 million po.

**[01:01:51.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000822)  
At yung iba pong mga licenses na nakukulekta po natin ay may 29 million po.

**[01:01:59.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000823)  
Sa kabuhan po.

**[01:02:00.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000824)  
Ang PCS po ay 175 million, 15,272 pesos po.

**[01:02:05.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000825)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:02:08.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000826)  
Board Resolution No. 12, Series of 2026, approved 15 million for Bakon, Ibungan, and Lupon.

**[01:02:19.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000827)  
While Board Resolution No. 15, Series of 2026, approved 10 million for Lawag City and Marilao.

**[01:02:27.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000828)  
So, what criteria?

**[01:02:30.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000829)  
Does the DDB use in selecting LGUs for these grants?

**[01:02:34.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000830)  
And how does it determine the 5 million allocation per LGU?

**[01:02:41.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000831)  
Ang unang criteria po, ito naman po lahat halos ng lugar ay maaring sabihin.

**[01:02:50.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000832)  
Pero meron po silang extensive drug problem na na-identify.

**[01:02:54.980] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000833)  
Una yun po.

**[01:02:55.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000834)  
Yung sa pangangailangan.

**[01:02:57.980] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000835)  
So, yun ang unahong criteria.

**[01:03:00.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000836)  
Ang criteria yung ginagamit po ng DDB, pagpili po ng mga LGU na bibigyan ng grants po nila.

**[01:03:08.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000837)  
Tapos, meron dapat silang mga komunidad na classified as affected by the Barangay Drug Clearing Program na pinatutupad po ng PIDEA.

**[01:03:20.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000838)  
Meron po ako ditong listahan po para sa taong 2026.

**[01:03:28.380] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000839)  
Labing apat po sila.

**[01:03:30.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000840)  
Na narito, pwede ko pong ibigay sa kagalang-galang po.

**[01:03:41.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000841)  
Bakon, tipungan, kapangan po.

**[01:03:48.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000842)  
And lupon.

**[01:03:49.580] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000843)  
Lubok.

**[01:03:52.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000844)  
Ano po yan eh.

**[01:03:54.380] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000845)  
So, special o particular po yung focus natin ginagawa.

**[01:04:00.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000846)  
Meron dahil nga po doon sa mariwan na cultivation.

**[01:04:09.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000847)  
Si lupo naman po ay dahil coastline po siya, kaya particular po tayo sa pagbabantay sa lugar na yun dahil doon sa shipment po na dumadaan o nakakapasok po doon sa lugar na yun po.

**[01:04:28.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000848)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[01:04:30.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000849)  
So, ito ang lupon.

**[01:04:30.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000850)  
Provide perhaps.

**[01:04:32.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000851)  
Where this lupon located, Mr. Speaker, District Sponsor?

**[01:04:36.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000852)  
Sa Dabao Oriental po ito, nakalocated po.

**[01:04:40.680] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000853)  
Ang kaharap po niya ay yung malaking dagat po ng Pacifico, yung Pacific Ocean po.

**[01:04:47.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000854)  
Kaya po, ang problema po natin ng drugs ay hindi ito parang street level lamang na ating tinutugunan.

**[01:04:58.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000855)  
Kundi, pati yung pagbabantay ng mga borders ay napakahalaga po.

**[01:05:04.460] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000856)  
Pati po yung paggamit ng mga mail parcels.

**[01:05:09.820] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000857)  
Dahil marami po ngayon, ngayong taon, tayo po.

**[01:05:14.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000858)  
Isa po yung sa Subico, yung 1,000.4 kilos.

**[01:05:25.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000859)  
1,000.4 kilos.

**[01:05:26.620] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000860)  
O.

**[01:05:27.520] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000861)  
1,000.4 kilos.

**[01:05:28.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000862)  
Yung 1,000.4 kilos po ng shabu na nakuha natin.

**[01:05:32.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000863)  
Tapos yung mga concealment na ginagawa po sa mga parcela.

**[01:05:37.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000864)  
Meron po tayong misang operation ng pideya na iniligay sa kahon na parang pangrigalong ikakasal yung galing Korea.

**[01:05:50.380] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000865)  
So, it's transnational yung problema po, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:05:55.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000866)  
Mr. Speaker, no.

**[01:05:57.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000867)  
Because here, it says Resolution No. 12, Series of 2026, approved 50 million.

**[01:06:02.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000868)  
Ito bang 50 million?

**[01:06:03.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000869)  
Hati-hati pa si Bakun, Kibungan, and Lupon.

**[01:06:06.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000870)  
So, tagpa 5 million lang sila.

**[01:06:08.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000871)  
Parang masyadong mali ito.

**[01:06:10.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000872)  
Especially in area of Lupon, wherein we're guarding the bus.

**[01:06:18.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000873)  
Kumbaga, ito malaki masyado ito.

**[01:06:20.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000874)  
So, would that be suffice enough?

**[01:06:22.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000875)  
Yung 5 million?

**[01:06:23.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000876)  
Yung mga 5 million po na nakikita at sinasabi.

**[01:06:27.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000877)  
Binibigay natin po na tulong sa mga munisipalidad na ito.

**[01:06:31.820] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000878)  
Ang totoo ay para sa infrastructure development.

**[01:06:36.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000879)  
Lalo na nung kanilang mga treatment and rehab facility.

**[01:06:40.660] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000880)  
Ibang bagay yung operations po.

**[01:06:43.580] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000881)  
At iba rin pong bagay yung personal complement, other resource, yung sustainability po.

**[01:06:55.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000882)  
Madalas po ay yung mga...

**[01:06:57.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000883)  
Local government units po natin ay nagbibigay din ang tulong.

**[01:07:02.680] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000884)  
At dahil devolved po yung services natin po.

**[01:07:10.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000885)  
At yung pati po ang PNP ay may resource din po dito na ibinibigay po.

**[01:07:16.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000886)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:07:17.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000887)  
Distinguished sponsor.

**[01:07:17.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000888)  
My follow-up question.

**[01:07:19.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000889)  
Following the SAGF system provided to LGUs,

**[01:07:23.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000890)  
what monitoring mechanism does...

**[01:07:27.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000891)  
may be used to ensure that these funded facilities are completed,

**[01:07:33.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000892)  
become operational,

**[01:07:34.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000893)  
and are able to sustain the rehabilitation services

**[01:07:38.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000894)  
via an initial SAGF grant?

**[01:07:41.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000895)  
So ano bang mechanism nyo sa pagmamonitor that this has been completed, no?

**[01:07:46.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000896)  
And it will be in use?

**[01:07:50.580] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000897)  
Ito po ay dinadownload sa mga local government unit.

**[01:07:55.580] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000898)  
Pag dinownload po ito,

**[01:07:57.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000899)  
at kung halimbawa ibibid nila,

**[01:08:01.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000900)  
so dapat po ay ano,

**[01:08:04.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000901)  
they would follow all the COA regulations,

**[01:08:10.140] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000902)  
procurement process,

**[01:08:12.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000903)  
at ang COA po ang nag-oobserba sa disbursement po ng pondo nito.

**[01:08:21.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000904)  
Kaya yung obligasyon na yun ay sinisiguro

**[01:08:27.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000905)  
na matutupad sa pamamagitan ng ating Commission on Audit po.

**[01:08:33.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000906)  
At meron din po tayong inter-agency monitoring body

**[01:08:40.160] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000907)  
na binubuo po ng apat na ahensya.

**[01:08:45.500] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000908)  
Dahil ang rehab at treatment ay nasa ilalim po ng pamamahala ng Department of Health.

**[01:08:51.920] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000909)  
Kasama po sila ang DOH.

**[01:08:54.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000910)  
Ang DILG po.

**[01:08:55.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000911)  
Naroon din po ang DDB.

**[01:08:59.600] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000912)  
At ang panghuli po ay yung Commission on Audit.

**[01:09:04.820] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000913)  
Talagang limang minyon lang po ang na-allocate.

**[01:09:08.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000914)  
Talagang maliit po.

**[01:09:10.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000915)  
Dahil po, iyon lamang po yung remittance

**[01:09:13.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000916)  
o nakukuha po nating pondo from PagCorp.

**[01:09:20.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000917)  
At ito po yung ginagamit natin ng DDB.

**[01:09:24.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000918)  
Thank you, Mr. Sponsor.

**[01:09:26.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000919)  
Mr. Speaker, katapos nyo po magbigay ng 5 million,

**[01:09:31.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000920)  
who will be responsible for funding the recurring personal, medical,

**[01:09:36.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000921)  
and operating costs of those facilities?

**[01:09:38.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000922)  
Once na nag-download po kayo, nagawa na po yun.

**[01:09:41.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000923)  
Of course, may recurring na gastusin yan.

**[01:09:45.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000924)  
Yung mga tauhan po.

**[01:09:46.980] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000925)  
Tama po yun.

**[01:09:48.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000926)  
So, saan ho mang gagaling yan?

**[01:09:49.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000927)  
Sino? LGU na ho ba?

**[01:09:50.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000928)  
Anong nangyayari po?

**[01:09:52.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000929)  
Dahil devolved service na po,

**[01:09:53.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000930)  
ang LGU na po ang namamahala po rito.

**[01:09:57.480] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000931)  
Pero gusto rin po natin bigyang diin sa pagkakataong ito,

**[01:10:01.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000932)  
ang DOH po ang pangunahing ahensya na mayroon hong obligasyon para panatilihin

**[01:10:10.780] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000933)  
dahil ang accreditation po ng mga rehab at treatment facilities

**[01:10:16.020] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000934)  
ay nanggagaling po sa DOH.

**[01:10:17.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000935)  
At ayon na rin po doon sa guidelines na meron tayo.

**[01:10:21.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000936)  
So, DOH po talaga ang namamahala.

**[01:10:23.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000937)  
Ang namamahala po doon sa mga rehab at treatment po.

**[01:10:29.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000938)  
Dahil nga po health-based na po tayo ayon dito sa ating ship-in policy na nangyayari ho.

**[01:10:36.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000939)  
Dahil tinuturing natin po yung mga drug user na hindi offender, hindi ho kriminal, kundi biktima na kailangang tulungan.

**[01:10:48.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000940)  
Tinignan ko po yung ating budget sa DOH.

**[01:10:52.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000941)  
Kung napondohan itong mga drug rehab at treatment facilities natin,

**[01:10:58.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000942)  
ang totoo po ay sa ngayon po ay hindi ito naging priority at walang pondong nakalaan po para sa taong ito.

**[01:11:08.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000943)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker and Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:11:11.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000944)  
Orders Resolution No. 14, Series of 2026 supports the institutionalization of CHED Memorandum Order No. 5, Series of 2026.

**[01:11:22.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000945)  
Which includes substance use prevention and early intervention in higher education.

**[01:11:30.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000946)  
Meanwhile, funding for development and implementation of the VOCACYs, information programs and production of IEC materials is reduced from

**[01:11:40.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000947)  
27.7 million in 2026 to 32.85 million in 2027.

**[01:11:51.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000948)  
With this reduction, what is the DDB's Fiscal Year 2027 target for the number of higher education,

**[01:12:02.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000949)  
SUCs, LUCs to be reached by its prevention and advocacy program?

**[01:12:08.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000950)  
And how does it compare with Fiscal Year 2026?

**[01:12:11.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000951)  
Bakit ako ba bumaba?

**[01:12:15.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000952)  
Bumaba po.

**[01:12:16.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000953)  
Oo. Bakit po? Anong reason bakit?

**[01:12:18.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000954)  
Bakit? Ano ba yung utilization rate nyo? Hindi ho ganun kataas?

**[01:12:23.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000955)  
Kasi usually, the DBM will give you more funding kung na-utilize nyo ang inyong pondo.

**[01:12:30.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000956)  
Kasi nakakabahala po ito.

**[01:12:31.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000957)  
Actually, ma'am, sir, Mr. Speaker, I support your advocacy, no?

**[01:12:35.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000958)  
I'm one for putting more money sa DDB because if you want to eradicate or obliterate the drugs,

**[01:12:45.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000959)  
kayo po ang na-support fraud.

**[01:12:47.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000960)  
So bakit po bumaba?

**[01:12:48.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000961)  
So something is wrong here, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:12:56.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000962)  
Dahil ang DDB po sa kanyang katayuan ngayon bilang coordinating agency,

**[01:13:04.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000963)  
ay hindi po siya katulad ng mga SUC or local university and colleges natin

**[01:13:13.820] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000964)  
at yung for higher education institution natin.

**[01:13:18.520] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000965)  
Kapag implement po, ano?

**[01:13:24.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000966)  
Basically, the budget of DDB is for support to operations.

**[01:13:30.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000967)  
Hindi ho siya isang lead implementing agency.

**[01:13:33.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000968)  
So in short, okay lang po kayo dyan?

**[01:13:35.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000969)  
Sa inpondo nyo from 47, it was downgraded to 32 million?

**[01:13:40.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000970)  
Okay.

**[01:13:40.620] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000971)  
Ano ho eh?

**[01:13:43.260] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000972)  
Ang mahalaga po ay yung mga research at yung data gathering.

**[01:13:48.520] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000973)  
Yung pag-coordinating with other agency po na makuha po yung tamang bilang.

**[01:13:55.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000974)  
Ayun po yung problema eh.

**[01:13:57.740] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000975)  
Gusto ho ng DDB magkaroon ng isang national dashboard na kung saan lahat ng mga impormasyon to,

**[01:14:05.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000976)  
datos na ito, mailalagay doon at makikita natin.

**[01:14:08.860] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000977)  
Halimbawa po, yung parang isang drug information portal ang gusto po ng DDB.

**[01:14:16.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000978)  
At yung...

**[01:14:18.520] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000979)  
Yung mga pondong naroon po sa mga nabanggit hong institution.

**[01:14:23.840] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000980)  
Yung mga SUC o HEI o LUC ay maibigay natin yung datos at yun ang matulungan ho.

**[01:14:34.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000981)  
With that, with that, ano, with that, with that.

**[01:14:36.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000982)  
The DDB is not asking for fund to implement yung mga reintegration program but simply to be able to collate all the data necessary.

**[01:14:48.520] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000983)  
Para alam natin kung kanino po maiaabot yung tulong na kailangan po.

**[01:14:54.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000984)  
Oo.

**[01:14:55.340] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000985)  
Saka kailangan po natin yung mga data na yun para mapabuti po natin yung...

**[01:15:00.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-000986)  
mga pulisiyan na meron po tayo.

**[01:15:02.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000987)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

Tulad lang ng mga nahuli na isang libong mahigit na kilo ng shabu sa Subico.

**[02:17:55.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001932)  
So, the source is international.

**[02:17:59.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001933)  
Yung po, best practices, yes, meron din po na ginagawa.

**[02:18:07.420] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001934)  
Yung collaboration po na ginagawa po natin, yun po yung mga nakakatulong po sa atin.

**[02:18:16.460] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001935)  
Transnational yung problema ng drugs.

**[02:18:19.340] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001936)  
Iba yung nagpipinance, may nagmamanufacture, may nagpipinance.

**[02:18:25.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001937)  
May nag-import, may magdi-distribute.

**[02:18:29.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001938)  
Lahat yan dapat ma-address po natin.

**[02:18:32.780] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001939)  
It can be addressed, I think, not only dahil meron tayong local net enforcement agency,

**[02:18:40.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001940)  
but importantly, yung international cooperation that we're getting.

**[02:18:47.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001941)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Sponsor, sa inyong kasagutan.

**[02:18:51.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001942)  
Sa huli po, nais ko lang sigurong pahayag na ang kinatawa,

**[02:18:55.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001943)  
kinatawang ito ay kaisa po ng DDB sa paghangad ng isang bayan na free of drugs

**[02:19:03.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001944)  
para sa kinabukasan ng ating mga anak at sa kinabukasan ng pamilyang Pilipino.

**[02:19:08.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001945)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:19:10.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001946)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:19:12.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001947)  
At, Mr. Speakers, I would like to note that lahat po na nagtanong ay in support of the DDB funding.

**[02:19:21.920] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001948)  
Salamat po.

**[02:19:25.440] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001949)  
Thank you very much, Majority Leader.

**[02:19:28.820] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001950)  
Mr. Speaker, I now move that we recognize Representative Presley C. De Jesus of Partylist Philreca.

**[02:19:40.280] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001951)  
Representative Jesus is recognized.

**[02:19:42.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001952)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:19:45.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001953)  
There being no more members for the minority who wishes to interpolate,

**[02:19:49.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001954)  
I move to terminate the period of sponsorship and debate for the budget of the...

**[02:19:55.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001955)  
the dangerous drug boards.

**[02:19:57.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001956)  
I so move, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:20:00.520] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001957)  
Thank you very much. What does the House say?

**[02:20:03.180] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001958)  
Mr. Speaker, I'm part of the majority.

**[02:20:05.740] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001959)  
We join the minority in the motion to terminate the interpolations of the proposed budget of the dangerous drugs board.

**[02:20:12.660] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001960)  
There is a joint motion to terminate the interpolations in the proposed budget of the dangerous drugs board.

**[02:20:19.660] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001961)  
Is there any objection?

**[02:20:21.640] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001962)  
The Chair hears none.

**[02:20:22.760] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001963)  
The interpolations on the proposed budget...

**[02:20:25.440] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001964)  
of the dangerous drugs board is hereby terminated.

**[02:20:28.780] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001965)  
Congratulations.

**[02:20:30.600] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001966)  
Majority Leader.

**[02:20:32.200] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001967)  
Mr. Speaker, the parliamentary status of House Bill No. 10858...

**[02:20:36.780] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001968)  
Salamat po, ma'am.

**[02:20:37.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001969)  
Thank you.

**[02:20:38.340] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001970)  
Thank you.

**[02:20:38.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001971)  
Thank you.

**[02:20:40.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-001972)  
Pasalamatan niyo si ma'am.

**[02:20:43.600] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001973)  
Again, Mr. Speaker...

**[02:20:44.760] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001974)  
Please proceed.

**[02:20:45.200] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001975)  
The parliamentary status of House Bill No. 10858 is that it is in the period of sponsorship and debate.

**[02:20:52.280] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001976)  
I now move to consider the proposed budget of the...

**[02:20:55.440] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001977)  
Philippine Drug Enforcement Agency.

**[02:20:57.780] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001978)  
Is there any objection?

**[02:20:59.300] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001979)  
The Chair hears none.

**[02:21:00.060] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001980)  
Motion is approved.

**[02:21:01.680] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001981)  
Majority Leader.

**[02:21:03.100] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001982)  
Mr. Speaker, may we acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials of the Philippine Drug Enforcement Agency.

**[02:21:10.260] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001983)  
They are...

**[02:21:11.260] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001984)  
Usec Isagani Neres,

**[02:21:13.960] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001985)  
General Rolando Miranda,

**[02:21:16.840] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001986)  
Director Ida Ferolino,

**[02:21:19.400] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001987)  
Director Ronnie Cudia,

**[02:21:21.620] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001988)  
Director Agnes Mandap,

**[02:21:24.180] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001989)  
Director Angela Saldana,

**[02:21:25.440] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001990)  
Director Emerson Margate,

**[02:21:29.500] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001991)  
Director Resty Lamonera,

**[02:21:32.240] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001992)  
Director Marla Bridget Galvin,

**[02:21:35.420] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001993)  
and Director Francis Del Valle.

**[02:21:37.300] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001994)  
We acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials from the Philippine Drug Enforcement Agency headed by Usec Isagani Neres.

**[02:21:47.460] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001995)  
Welcome to the House of Representatives.

**[02:21:49.960] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-001996)  
Majority Leader.

**[02:21:51.320] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001997)  
Mr. Speaker, I now move to recognize representatives...

**[02:21:55.440] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001998)  
Marcellino Marci R. Teodoro

**[02:21:57.760] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-001999)  
of the 1st District of the City of Marikina,

**[02:22:01.540] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-002000)  
Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations,

**[02:22:04.480] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-002001)  
to answer questions on the proposed budget of the Philippine Drug Enforcement Agency.

**[02:22:10.700] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-002002)  
Representative Teodoro is recognized. Please proceed.

**[02:22:13.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002003)  
Mr. Speaker, my distinguished colleagues,

**[02:22:17.200] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002004)  
when we talk about illegal drugs, we can easily get lost in numbers.

**[02:22:21.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002005)  
Kilosis, operations conducted,

**[02:22:24.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002006)  
personalities arrested.

**[02:22:27.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002007)  
But for an ordinary Filipino,

**[02:22:29.920] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002008)  
the concern is much simpler.

**[02:22:32.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002009)  
Ligtas ba ang anak ko?

**[02:22:34.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002010)  
Ligtas ba ang komunidad namin?

**[02:22:36.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002011)  
May ginagawa ba ang gobyerno

**[02:22:38.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002012)  
bago pa umabot ang droga sa aming barangay?

**[02:22:42.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002013)  
That is the public purpose behind the budget

**[02:22:45.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002014)  
of the Philippine Drug Enforcement Agency.

**[02:22:49.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002015)  
And the problem PIDEA is dealing with today

**[02:22:52.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002016)  
is very different from what it was

**[02:22:54.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002017)  
many years ago.

**[02:22:57.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002018)  
Illegal drugs are no longer moving only from one street to another.

**[02:23:02.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002019)  
Drug syndicates now operate across borders.

**[02:23:06.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002020)  
They use commercial cargo planes, airports, international parcels,

**[02:23:12.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002021)  
ports, coastlines, and the open sea.

**[02:23:16.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002022)  
Nagbabago sila ng modus, nagbabago sila ng paraan ng pagtatago.

**[02:23:22.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002023)  
Kapag nasanay na ang gobyerno,

**[02:23:24.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002024)  
sa isang modus, lilipat sila sa iba.

**[02:23:27.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002025)  
So the government must be able to adapt just as quickly.

**[02:23:32.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002026)  
That is where PIDEA has a very specific role.

**[02:23:36.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002027)  
The law mandates PIDEA to conduct anti-drug operations and intelligence work.

**[02:23:41.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002028)  
It identifies high-value personalities and organizations.

**[02:23:46.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002029)  
It coordinates interdiction efforts with other agencies.

**[02:23:51.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002030)  
Its job is to make it harder

**[02:23:53.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002031)  
for illegal drugs to enter, circulate, and reach our communities.

**[02:24:00.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002032)  
And we have seen how serious that task has become.

**[02:24:04.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002033)  
PIDEA itself has identified airports, seaports, coastlines,

**[02:24:10.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002034)  
and mail and parcel systems as major entry points for illegal drugs.

**[02:24:17.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002035)  
It also documented the June 2025 interdiction off Subic,

**[02:24:22.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002036)  
involving approximately 1,484.65 kilograms of Shabu.

**[02:24:30.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002037)  
This is no longer a street-level drug crime.

**[02:24:36.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002038)  
Kapag ganito kalaki ang shipment, malinaw may organisasyon sa likod nito.

**[02:24:43.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002039)  
May pera, may logistics, may intelligence, may network.

**[02:24:47.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002040)  
For fiscal year 2027,

**[02:24:50.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002041)  
PIDEA is proposed to receive 4.41 billion in new general appropriations.

**[02:24:58.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002042)  
For fiscal year 2026,

**[02:25:00.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002043)  
the amount was 4.37 billion pesos,

**[02:25:06.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002044)  
or approximately 1.1% increase

**[02:25:12.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002045)  
in the amount of 47.77 million.

**[02:25:18.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002046)  
This is not an extravagant request.

**[02:25:21.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002047)  
It is a measured allocation for an agency

**[02:25:24.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002048)  
whose responsibilities are becoming more complex.

**[02:25:28.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002049)  
And the way the budget is structured also tells us something important.

**[02:25:34.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002050)  
The Dangerous Drug Supply Reduction and Suppression Program

**[02:25:37.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002051)  
is proposed at approximately 4.06 billion,

**[02:25:42.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002052)  
while anti-drug operations account for approximately 3.75 billion.

**[02:25:47.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002053)  
At the same time,

**[02:25:49.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002054)  
capital outlay declines from 783.87 million in fiscal year 2026

**[02:25:58.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002055)  
to 640.59 million in fiscal year 2027.

**[02:26:06.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002056)  
So this is not simply about buying more equipment.

**[02:26:10.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002057)  
Mas malinaw ang direction, mas nakatutok sa operations.

**[02:26:15.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002058)  
And there are results that justify continued support.

**[02:26:18.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002059)  
In 2025, PIDEA reported that 84% of its PIDEA-initiated arrests

**[02:26:25.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002060)  
involved high-value targets,

**[02:26:28.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002061)  
compared with a target of 60%.

**[02:26:31.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002062)  
It conducted 696 high-impact operations

**[02:26:36.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002063)  
against a target of 500.

**[02:26:39.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002064)  
And 73.88% of drug-related information and reports

**[02:26:44.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002065)  
acted upon resulted in actual anti-drug operations.

**[02:26:49.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002066)  
Those are encouraging figures.

**[02:26:54.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002067)  
Those results show that PIDEA is increasingly directing its work

**[02:27:03.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002068)  
toward higher-value targets and intelligence-driven enforcement.

**[02:27:08.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002069)  
Ang mahalga ngayon ay ituloy at palalimin ang nagawa na.

**[02:27:14.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002070)  
The next step is to build on those gains

**[02:27:17.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002071)  
by reaching deeper into the organizations that finance,

**[02:27:21.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002072)  
import, and distribute illegal drugs.

**[02:27:24.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002073)  
It means identifying trafficking routes earlier.

**[02:27:29.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002074)  
It means strengthening interdiction before shipment can reach our communities.

**[02:27:35.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002075)  
Sa madaling salita, hindi lang paramihin ang huli,

**[02:27:39.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002076)  
ang mahalaga ay kung mas napapahina natin ang sindikato,

**[02:27:43.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002077)  
at mas napipigilan ang pagpasok ng droga.

**[02:27:47.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002078)  
That is how the agency's present gains can be translated

**[02:27:51.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002079)  
into a more sustained disruption of organized drug networks.

**[02:27:57.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002080)  
That is the result Congress should continue to support.

**[02:28:00.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002081)  
And one reason continued support matters

**[02:28:04.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002082)  
is that PIDEA becomes more effective

**[02:28:07.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002083)  
when its operations are integrated with other agencies responsible

**[02:28:12.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002084)  
for our borders and national security.

**[02:28:16.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002085)  
Its work is strongest when that coordination is immediate and operational.

**[02:28:22.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002086)  
We are an archipelago. Napakahaba ng ating coastline.

**[02:28:26.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002087)  
Marami tayong ports of entry. Maraming lugar na kailangan bantayan.

**[02:28:30.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002088)  
So PIDEA must work closely with the Bureau of Customs,

**[02:28:34.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002089)  
the Coast Guard, the Navy, military intelligence, airport authorities,

**[02:28:41.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002090)  
local law enforcement, and our international partners.

**[02:28:46.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002091)  
Hindi pwedeng may impormasyon ang isang ahensya pero natitigil lang sa opisina.

**[02:28:53.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002092)  
Kung may nakita ang customs, kailangan makarating agad sa PIDEA.

**[02:28:58.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002093)  
Kung may maritime threat, kailangan alam agad ng Coast Guard

**[02:29:02.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002094)  
at ng iba pang security agencies. Kung mabilis kumilos ang sindikato,

**[02:29:07.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002095)  
mas mabilis dapat ang coordination ng gobyerno.

**[02:29:11.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002096)  
That is part of what we are funding.

**[02:29:15.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002097)  
Mr. Speaker, this is why I believe the proposed budget deserves our support.

**[02:29:21.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002098)  
With this support, we can reasonably expect disciplined intelligence work.

**[02:29:25.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002099)  
We can expect stronger interdiction. We can expect closer coordination.

**[02:29:29.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002100)  
And we should expect, more importantly, measurable results against major drug organizations.

**[02:29:36.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002101)  
Sa dulo, simple lang naman ang gusto nating makita.

**[02:29:40.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002102)  
Mas mahirap makapasok ang droga. Mas mahirap gumalaw ang sindikato.

**[02:29:45.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002103)  
Mas ligtas ang pamilyang Pilipino. Yan ang sukatan ng pondong ito.

**[02:29:50.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002104)  
At kung iyon ang direksyon ng PIDEA, then this House has every reason

**[02:29:56.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002105)  
to give the agency the resources it needs.

**[02:30:00.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002106)  
to continue its work.

**[02:30:02.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002107)  
Dahil sa bawat shipment na hindi nakapasok,

**[02:30:04.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002108)  
may komunidad na hindi naaabot,

**[02:30:07.580] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002109)  
sa bawat sindikatong napahina,

**[02:30:09.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002110)  
may pamilyang at kababayan tayong maliligtas.

**[02:30:14.620] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002111)  
For these reasons, Mr. Speaker,

**[02:30:18.260] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002112)  
I rise to sponsor and respectfully urge this House

**[02:30:22.160] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002113)  
to approve the proposed Fiscal Year 2027 Budget

**[02:30:26.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002114)  
of the Philippine Drug Enforcement Agency.

**[02:30:28.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002115)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:30:58.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002116)  
Mr. Speaker, under the Fiscal Year 2027 NEP,

**[02:31:05.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002117)  
FIDEA is allocated 500 million in confidential expenses

**[02:31:09.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002118)  
under Anti-Drug Operations, MOOE,

**[02:31:14.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002119)  
the same amount appropriated in the 2026 Fiscal Year GAA.

**[02:31:20.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002120)  
This represents approximately one-third of FIDEA's proposed 1.5 billion MOE,

**[02:31:28.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002121)  
for Fiscal Year 2027.

**[02:31:32.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002122)  
What specific performance indicators does FIDEA use

**[02:31:37.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002123)  
to assess whether this 500 million allocation

**[02:31:39.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002124)  
is producing actionable intelligence

**[02:31:43.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002125)  
and high-impact operation,

**[02:31:45.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002126)  
and how does the agency coordinate these intelligence activities

**[02:31:49.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002127)  
with PNP, NICA, and Amphors of the Philippines

**[02:31:54.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002128)  
to avoid duplication?

**[02:31:58.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002129)  
It's true, Mr. Speaker,

**[02:32:01.840] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002130)  
Fiscal Year 2027 NEP provides 500 million in confidential expenses,

**[02:32:08.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002131)  
the same amount reflected for 2025 and 2026.

**[02:32:15.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002132)  
We very well know that we cannot require disclosure

**[02:32:19.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002133)  
of confidential sources or sensitive operational details in plenary.

**[02:32:24.980] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002134)  
However,

**[02:32:27.900] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002135)  
FIDEA may be required to provide confidential information

**[02:32:28.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002136)  
to demonstrate aggregate results attributable to intelligence work,

**[02:32:35.640] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002137)  
such as actionable intelligence-developed intelligence-driven interdictions.

**[02:32:42.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002138)  
On this aspect, Mr. Speaker,

**[02:32:46.540] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002139)  
high-impact operations and disruption of major organizations,

**[02:32:51.260] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002140)  
the NEP reports that in 2025,

**[02:32:53.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002141)  
1,471,

**[02:32:57.020] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002142)  
of 1,991

**[02:32:58.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002143)  
drug-related information reports

**[02:33:02.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002144)  
are acted upon

**[02:33:04.340] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002145)  
or 73.88 resulted in anti-drug operation.

**[02:33:10.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002146)  
Admittedly, not 100% of the drug-related information

**[02:33:17.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002147)  
are acted favorably, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:33:28.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002148)  
I have here, Mr. Speaker,

**[02:33:31.760] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002149)  
the outcome indicator of the agency

**[02:33:37.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002150)  
based on the performance targets and accomplishments.

**[02:33:42.800] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002151)  
Perhaps, Mr. Speaker,

**[02:33:44.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002152)  
on a later period,

**[02:33:45.540] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002153)  
I could provide this copy to the good interpolator.

**[02:33:50.380] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002154)  
In terms of percentage decrease in drug personalities,

**[02:33:56.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002155)  
the target actually is 10%.

**[02:33:58.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002156)  
As set forth in this year's budget,

**[02:34:05.120] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002157)  
we were able to arrest 173 personalities

**[02:34:11.400] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002158)  
totaling to accounting for 10.18% of the target.

**[02:34:19.320] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002159)  
So we exceeded a bit from the target.

**[02:34:21.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002160)  
But in terms of high-value targets arrested,

**[02:34:28.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002161)  
our target,

**[02:34:31.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002162)  
as I have mentioned earlier,

**[02:34:33.320] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002163)  
is 60% of the total arrests.

**[02:34:36.540] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002164)  
And we were able to have an arrest of 2,368

**[02:34:43.160] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002165)  
out of the 2,819 on the list.

**[02:34:50.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002166)  
That is 84% accomplishment.

**[02:34:56.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002167)  
And the number of high-impact operations

**[02:34:58.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002168)  
conducted,

**[02:35:00.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002169)  
as I have mentioned in my sponsorship speech,

**[02:35:04.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002170)  
the 500 operations that we targeted this year,

**[02:35:08.920] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002171)  
we already exceeded that

**[02:35:10.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002172)  
in the number of 696 operations

**[02:35:13.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002173)  
as we speak now.

**[02:35:15.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002174)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker,

**[02:35:17.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002175)  
Distinguished Sponsor.

**[02:35:18.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002176)

**[02:35:27.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002179)

**[02:35:31.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002181)

**[02:35:56.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002189)  
so that drug laws,

**[02:35:58.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002190)  
drug use or drug use prevalence

**[02:36:01.200] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002191)

**[02:36:08.280] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002192)  
Ang composition po nito ay

**[02:36:10.680] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002193)

**[02:36:17.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002195)

**[02:36:18.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002196)

**[02:36:21.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002197)

**[02:36:28.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002200)  
Ang isa pa po nakasama po rito

**[02:36:32.740] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002201)  
dahil mahalaga po yung intelligence work

**[02:36:35.660] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002202)  
at misan, ito yung nako-compromise

**[02:36:38.620] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002203)

**[02:36:41.000] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002204)

**[02:36:43.440] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002205)  
At sa operation,

**[02:36:46.260] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002206)  
ay katulong din po natin

**[02:36:48.080] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002207)  
yung ilang mga units po natin

**[02:36:50.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002208)  
from the armed forces of the Philippines.

**[02:36:53.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002209)  
Yes, po.

**[02:36:55.600] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002210)  
Meron din po tayong memorandum,

**[02:36:58.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002211)

**[02:37:00.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002212)  
sa pagitan po ng PIDEA at

**[02:37:09.980] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002214)

**[02:37:14.320] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002215)

**[02:37:17.340] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002216)  
para may iwasan po yung mga

**[02:37:19.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002217)  
duplication po ng operation

**[02:37:21.020] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002218)  
o kaya yung mga

**[02:37:22.740] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002219)  
missing counter na

**[02:37:24.940] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002220)  
tinatawag, pero hindi pa rin

**[02:37:27.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002221)

**[02:37:28.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002222)

**[02:37:38.480] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002225)

**[02:37:40.320] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002226)  
sa pagitan po ng mga drug

**[02:37:42.220] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002227)  
law enforcement units po natin.

**[02:37:46.820] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002228)

**[02:37:49.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002229)

**[02:37:52.160] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002230)

**[02:37:54.960] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002231)

**[02:37:58.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002232)  
At

**[02:37:59.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002233)  
yun po yung

**[02:38:03.520] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002234)

**[02:38:06.220] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002235)

**[02:38:14.780] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002239)  
Dahil misan, hindi ho nagkaroon

**[02:38:16.200] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002240)

**[02:38:17.840] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002241)

**[02:38:20.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002242)

**[02:38:21.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002243)

**[02:38:25.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002244)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[02:38:26.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002245)

**[02:38:34.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002250)

**[02:38:41.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002254)  
PNP, PIDEA, and

**[02:38:44.500] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002255)

**[02:38:49.880] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002257)

**[02:38:56.220] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002259)  
Ang Bureau of Customs po,

**[02:38:58.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002260)  
dahil ho transnational

ang problema po ng

**[02:39:02.220] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002262)

At ang DOJ
