**[02:39:08.480] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002265)  
Dahil ho doon sa prosecution

**[02:39:10.160] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002266)  
aspect at handling

**[02:39:12.220] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002267)  
ng mga

**[02:39:14.040] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002268)  
ano po,

**[02:39:16.300] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002269)  
yung mga ebidensya po natin.

**[02:39:19.020] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002270)

**[02:39:22.320] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002272)

**[02:39:24.560] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002273)

**[02:39:26.020] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002274)

**[02:39:28.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002275)

**[02:39:29.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002276)

**[02:40:38.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002304)

**[02:40:49.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002305)

**[02:40:53.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002306)

**[02:40:55.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002307)

**[02:40:58.720] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002308)

**[02:41:01.500] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002309)

**[02:41:04.060] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002310)

**[02:41:10.660] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002312)

**[02:41:21.520] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002315)

**[02:41:29.100] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002317)

**[02:41:31.340] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002318)

**[02:41:38.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002320)

**[02:41:44.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002321)

**[02:41:46.460] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002322)

**[02:41:50.140] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002323)

**[02:42:05.540] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002328)

**[03:00:06.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002636)

**[03:01:23.240] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002644)

**[03:01:25.360] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002645)

**[03:01:29.020] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002647)  
seizure of asset maliit po

**[03:01:31.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002648)  
dahil kadalas ang mga foreigner po.

**[03:01:33.660] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002649)  
So may proseso po tayong

**[03:01:35.180] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002650)  
binadaanan.

**[03:01:38.660] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002651)  
Tapos

**[03:01:39.340] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002652)

**[03:01:41.700] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002653)

**[03:01:45.500] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002654)

**[03:01:46.840] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002655)  
nakapending na

**[03:01:49.540] Rep. Marcelino "Marcy" R. Teodoro** (asr-002656)  
case po doon.

Bigyan ko po kayo ng halimbawa kung ilan po

**[03:17:42.920] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002860)  
to the Commission on Audit for

**[03:17:45.140] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002861)  
post-audit. Any disallowed

**[03:17:47.240] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002862)  
amount remains the Chairman's personal

**[03:17:49.400] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002863)  
accountability.

**[03:17:51.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002864)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:17:53.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002865)  
Under GAP

**[03:17:54.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002866)  
Board Resolution Number

**[03:17:56.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002867)  
2026-08,

**[03:17:58.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002868)  
The Board discontinued the off-track betting OTB sticker program which issued 166 OTB stickers in 2025

**[03:18:09.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002869)  
after determining that the program was neither cost-effective nor operationally necessary.

**[03:18:15.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002870)  
What were the annual costs of the sticker program and what savings does GAAP expect from its discontinuation?

**[03:18:28.940] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002871)  
Actually, Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, regarding that question, it is, we can say, a material expense because it only cost 15,000 pesos, Your Honor.

**[03:18:58.940] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002872)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:19:00.120] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002873)  
Around 95 pesos per sticker.

**[03:19:04.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002874)  
With the stickers no longer being used, what specific verification measures will GAAP inspectors and local authorities use in the field

**[03:19:13.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002875)  
to immediately distinguish licensed OTB stations from unauthorized betting operations?

**[03:19:28.940] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002876)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, a licensed OTB will have business permits from LGUs and BIR registrations.

**[03:19:51.580] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002877)  
They can be easily seen by GAAP inspectors, Your Honor.

**[03:19:58.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002878)  
Mr. Speaker, under Special Provision 1 of the GAAP NEP, GAAP is authorized to utilize 9 million from its special account in the general fund

**[03:20:13.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002879)  
representing its 3% share from gross rate receipts and broadcasting rights under PD 871.

**[03:20:21.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002880)  
To augment its MOE, GAAP's 2025 financial report states that

**[03:20:28.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002881)  
following COA observations and BTR circulars, it conducted a cost management seminar and established three separate bank accounts at Philippine Veterans Bank

**[03:20:39.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002882)  
for its 3% share, regular license fees, and trust funds.

**[03:20:45.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002883)  
What specific COA observations or cost management issues led to the requirement of separate bank accounts

**[03:20:53.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002884)  
and what corrective measures has GAAP implemented to ensure that these special account funds can be used?

**[03:20:58.940] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002889)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, the cash management requirement for the Games and Amusement Board

**[03:21:07.280] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002890)  
to establish three separate bank accounts at the Philippine Veterans Bank stemmed from Commission and Audit.

**[03:21:15.120] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002891)  
Observations regarding commingling of public funds and the lack of proper accounting segmentation.

**[03:21:22.020] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002892)  
Prior to this directive, different streams of agency revenue, especially GAAP's 3% share from gross gate receipts,

**[03:21:28.780] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002893)  
and broadcasting rights under PD 871, its regular license fees and trust funds were not cleanly separated at the processing and reporting levels.

**[03:21:42.280] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002894)  
This lacked compliance with Update Bureau of Treasury, circulars, and exposed the agency to potential challenges in auditing specific special accounts in the general funds allocations.

**[03:21:54.020] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002895)  
To address this observation, GAAP implemented several target...

**[03:21:58.780] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002896)  
GAAP created three dedicated accounts at the Philippine Veterans Bank to completely isolate its 3% share, regular collection, and trust fund management to eliminate commingling and create a transparent, automated, and auditable system.

**[03:22:19.900] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002897)  
GAB's Finance and Administrative Division organized a mandatory cash management seminar for all bonded and collecting officers to instill

**[03:22:34.280] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002910)  
strict compliance with update COA and BTR's protocols.

**[03:22:39.800] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002911)  
Collecting officers were trained and detailed categorizing protocols.

**[03:22:45.920] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002912)  
Reports are now structured to mandate separate entries from funds types

**[03:22:52.100] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002913)  
such as account 151 for the 3% share versus regular license collections,

**[03:22:59.980] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002914)  
simplifying the oversight and verification trail.

**[03:23:03.060] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002915)  
Your Honor.

**[03:23:05.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002916)  
Mr. Speaker, as of the latest available audit,

**[03:23:08.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002917)  
are all 3% gate receipt and broadcasting rights shares due from professional leagues

**[03:23:14.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002918)  
fully remitted, recorded, and supported by audit documentation?

**[03:23:20.520] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002919)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:23:24.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002920)  
Mr. Speaker, GAB requires medical examinations and clearances

**[03:23:30.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002921)  
as part of its regulation of professional combat sports.

**[03:23:34.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002922)  
To protect the health and safety of athletes,

**[03:23:38.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002923)  
GAB accomplishment report also highlights partnership with LGUs

**[03:23:44.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002924)  
to provide free medical services to resident athletes.

**[03:23:50.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002925)  
Given the cost of these medical requirements

**[03:23:52.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002926)  
and GAB's limited MOOE for professional sports supervision,

**[03:23:58.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002927)  
what mechanisms does GAB have to ensure that indigent professional fighters

**[03:24:04.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002928)  
can obtain the required medical and neurological clearances,

**[03:24:09.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002929)  
regardless of whether the LGU has an existing partnership with GAB?

**[03:24:16.200] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002930)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor,

**[03:24:18.040] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002931)  
the Games and Amusement Board utilizes a national-level institutional partnership

**[03:24:23.720] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002932)  
with the Department of DOH, Your Honor.

**[03:24:27.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002933)  
And for my last question, Mr. Speaker,

**[03:24:31.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002934)  
this is my sponsor.

**[03:24:33.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002935)  
Of the...

**[03:24:34.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002936)  
3,034 licenses and 1,392 permits issued in 2025,

**[03:24:42.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002937)  
how many involve combat sports athletes who were denied or deferred

**[03:24:48.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002938)  
because of medical, neurological, or drug testing results

**[03:24:53.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002939)  
for athletes who are injured during sanctioned events?

**[03:24:58.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002940)  
What financial or medical assistance mechanism is available

**[03:25:02.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002941)  
for post-fight?

**[03:25:04.280] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002942)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor,

**[03:25:07.180] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002943)  
under GAB's amended rules and regulations,

**[03:25:10.180] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002944)  
the Board is legally authorized to maintain and dispense the GAB Boxers Relief Fund.

**[03:25:15.200] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002945)  
This internal mechanism is specifically designed

**[03:25:19.580] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002946)  
to provide emergency financial assistance and relief to professional fighters

**[03:25:24.560] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002947)  
who are injured in the line of duty or find themselves indigent

**[03:25:29.220] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-002948)  
due to the physical aftermath of a sanctioned bout, Your Honor.

**[03:25:34.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002949)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:25:38.140] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-002950)  
Thank you.

**[03:25:39.320] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-002951)  
With just 12 minutes, that was a very good interpolation.

Majority Leader.

Mr. Speaker, again, I would like to request that we...

**[03:25:48.420] Rep. Ernesto "Ernix" Dionisio Jr.** (asr-002954)

**[03:28:31.940] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003014)  
to our distinguished colleagues,

Majority Leader.

**[03:29:31.940] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-003032)  
I move to recognize Representative Presley De Jesus

**[03:29:48.560] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-003033)  
from Pilreka Partylist

**[03:29:50.560] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-003034)  
to interpolate the sponsor

**[03:29:52.000] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-003035)  
on the proposed budget

**[03:29:54.940] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-003036)  
of the Philippine Sports Commission.

**[03:29:56.920] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003037)  
Representative De Jesus

**[03:29:58.080] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003038)  
is recognized to interpolate.

**[03:30:00.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003039)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker and Distinguished Sponsor, Pong Jerry Espina.

**[03:30:08.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003040)  
Mr. Speaker, the Philippine Sports Commission, which is very close to my heart, is mandated to set the priorities and direction of national sports promotion with emphasis on expanding grassroots sports participation.

**[03:30:25.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003041)  
However, under Fiscal Year 2027 National Expedition Program, the financial assistance to athletes, including community sports development, sports spells, and sports activities was reduced from 545 million in 2026 to only 45 million, a 91.7% reduction,

**[03:30:51.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003042)  
or 499 million.

**[03:30:55.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003043)  
The 150 million National Grassroots Sports Development Program and 300 million Sports Tourism Program in 2026 were likewise not carried over into the Fiscal Year 2027 NEP.

**[03:31:11.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003044)  
Meanwhile, the National Sports for All Grassroots Center Police Program was reduced from 80 million to 63 million.

**[03:31:20.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003045)  
Given the PSC's statutory mandate to expand grassroots participation,

**[03:31:25.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003046)  
how does Commission intend to maintain or expand grassroots sports activities and participation despite this significant reduction in funding?

**[03:31:37.460] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003047)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, under Section 6 and Section 26 of RA 6847, the PSC is mandated to supplement regular general appropriations from utilizing the National Sports Development Fund.

**[03:31:55.180] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003048)  
To finance the staging of national games, grassroots talents identification, and community compensations.

**[03:32:11.300] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003049)  
Furthermore, the PSC's strategic framework relies on inter-agency cooperation with local government units

**[03:32:18.880] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003050)  
and local physical fitness and sports development councils under Executive Orders 63 and 64.

**[03:32:25.180] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003051)  
to roll out community-level sports-for-all initiatives, Your Honor.

**[03:32:32.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003052)  
Mr. Speaker, on the onset, recently, as you know, Alex Ayala became famous.

**[03:32:40.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003053)  
Never in the history of the Philippines that we have a tennis player.

**[03:32:44.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003054)  
First time po, no?

**[03:32:47.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003055)  
At sa kanyang mga interview, mukhang mahirapan talaga ang ating mga atlit dito sa bansa

**[03:32:53.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003056)  
because tennis is new to us, no?

**[03:32:57.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003057)  
Because as you know, Philippine is very tayo, masyado tayong dedicated sa basketball.

**[03:33:03.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003058)  
So, with the new kind of atlit, no?

**[03:33:08.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003059)  
In the presence of Alex Ayala, ano ba pwede natin itulong?

**[03:33:12.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003060)  
Lalo ngayon, para sa atin, ang tennis sport ay hindi naman ganun kagrabe.

**[03:33:18.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003061)  
Sabi nga nila, ang tennis daw sa Pilipinas, pang mayaman, no?

**[03:33:21.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003062)  
I like basketball.

**[03:33:23.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003063)  
Yung volleyball, or kung mga larong pang ordinary, kaya.

**[03:33:28.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003064)  
But tennis requires ang new mga equipments, medyo mahal.

**[03:33:34.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003065)  
At kailangan magkaroon ka ng sponsor.

**[03:33:37.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003066)  
If not, kung kaya ng magulang mo, kagaya nila Alex Ayala, ay napadala sa Spain, no?

**[03:33:44.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003067)  
Sa Rapa Academy.

**[03:33:47.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003068)  
So, ngayon po, ano nakikita ng Philippine Sports Commission dito sa tennis?

**[03:33:53.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003069)  
Na ngayon ay talagang sikat na sikat na medyo umangat ang ating bansa because of Alex Ayala.

**[03:34:03.340] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003070)  
Well, sangayon ako sa inyong pahayag, Ms. Your Honor.

**[03:34:11.440] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003071)  
Sa katunayan, based kay chairman, we will continue to build the regional training centers

**[03:34:22.420] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003072)  
focusing on...

**[03:34:23.620] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003073)  
Tennis, and some facilities to help our athletes.

**[03:34:31.500] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003074)  
Yun po, Your Honor.

**[03:34:33.540] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003075)  
We will build more training centers for tennis.

**[03:34:38.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003076)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:34:40.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003077)  
Masakit man tanggapin, no?

**[03:34:42.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003078)  
Tayo masyado hook up sa basketball.

**[03:34:45.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003079)  
Siguro, the next hundred years, I don't think magcha-champion tayo, no?

**[03:34:49.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003080)  
Kasi we're not built athletically.

**[03:34:52.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003081)  
Wala tayong height, no?

**[03:34:55.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003082)  
Unlike yung mga foreign na talaga.

**[03:34:57.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003083)  
So, ako siguro, Mr. Chair, no?

**[03:34:59.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003084)  
We can't concentrate boxing, tennis, and now, kahit sana yung golf.

**[03:35:03.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003085)  
Kaso, ang golf, again, is sabi nila, larong pang mayaman.

**[03:35:07.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003086)  
Do we see a future...

**[03:35:09.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003087)  
This is your sponsor, of course, the help of Chairperson Pato Gregorio.

**[03:35:18.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003088)  
Na ito bang golf natin?

**[03:35:21.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003089)  
Pwede ba tayo magkaroon?

**[03:35:22.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003090)  
Pwede ba tayo magkaroon ng academy rin?

**[03:35:24.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003091)  
Na kagaya ng Thailand, kagaya ng Vietnam, nagsisimula na siya.

**[03:35:29.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003092)  
Kasi dito, Mr. Chair, dito ko lang nakikita na aasenso tayo, eh.

**[03:35:33.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003093)  
Tennis, golf, and boxing, no?

**[03:35:36.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003094)  
Not disrespecting basketball.

**[03:35:38.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003095)  
Eh, kahit anong gawin natin, hindi tayo aasenso, no?

**[03:35:41.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003096)  
Sa basketball.

**[03:35:42.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003097)  
Ito lang nakikita ko that will bring us yung tinatawag na promised land.

**[03:35:47.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003098)  
What can you say, Mr. Speaker, this is your sponsor pagdating naman sa larong golf?

**[03:35:52.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003099)  
Ito.

**[03:35:52.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003100)  
Can we build also, or we started already building yung tinatawag nating academy natin?

**[03:36:00.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003101)  
Well, Mr. Speaker, Your Honor,

**[03:36:03.400] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003102)  
Philippine Sports Commission is now focused on medal-rich Olympic sports.

**[03:36:11.740] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003103)  
Kagaya ng sinabi mo na pinipilit natin yung basketball,

**[03:36:16.220] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003104)  
pero ilan taon na tayong talagang nakikita natin na hindi talaga uubra.

**[03:36:22.920] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003105)  
So with that, we have to concentrate or focus dun sa mga sports na talagang alam natin

**[03:36:28.060] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003106)  
may kakayahan ang Pilipino para mag-excel dun sa gano'ng klaseng sports.

**[03:36:35.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003107)  
At ang ginagawa po ng Philippine Sports Commission ay gano'n po.

**[03:36:40.220] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003108)  
Your Honor?

**[03:36:41.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003109)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor, no?

**[03:36:45.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003110)  
Sana supportahan natin.

**[03:36:46.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003111)  
Do you have any numbers, no?

**[03:36:50.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003112)  
Na tingin nyo?

**[03:36:51.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003113)  
Kasi ngayon, isa lang ang ating...

**[03:36:52.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003114)  
Sa lady golfer natin, if I'm not mistaken, si Pagdanganan ata, no?

**[03:36:59.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003115)  
Yung lady golfer natin.

**[03:37:02.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003116)  
Ito, nakapasok siya ngayon, naglalaro siya ngayon sa Florida or something, no?

**[03:37:06.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003117)  
And of course, Alex Ayala.

**[03:37:07.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003118)  
Do you see any future natin na nakikitaan nyo po ng talent that we can develop?

**[03:37:15.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003119)  
At yung bang pondo nyo ngayon, naka-allocate para dyan?

**[03:37:19.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003120)  
O itong ating pondo ay allocated already?

**[03:37:22.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003121)  
Sa ibang sports?

**[03:37:25.760] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003122)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, si Ryan Maliksi.

**[03:37:31.100] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003123)  
Malaki po ang future niya sa larangan ng golf.

**[03:37:34.600] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003124)  
Bata pa po siya at nagpapakita siya ng kausayan sa larangan ng golf.

**[03:37:40.400] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003125)  
So, sa tingin ko, ang PSC ay tututukan to.

**[03:37:45.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003126)  
Your Honor?

**[03:37:46.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003127)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:37:47.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003128)  
Sana tutukan natin yung makikita natin na kaya nilang mag-champion.

**[03:37:52.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003129)  
Tennis and golf.

**[03:37:55.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003130)  
Mr. Chair, with that, no?

**[03:37:56.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003131)  
I have a lot of questions, but again, it's more on numbers, no?

**[03:38:01.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003132)  
But again, that's the most important.

**[03:38:03.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003133)  
Maybe this one will be my last question.

**[03:38:06.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003134)  
Mr. Speaker, under PSC sports for all framework,

**[03:38:09.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003135)  
inclusive sports programs include women in sports,

**[03:38:14.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003136)  
para sports or the Pilipinas para games,

**[03:38:17.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003137)  
and indigenous people's games.

**[03:38:21.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003138)  
Given the overall reduction in funding,

**[03:38:22.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003139)  
for grassroots and sports for all programs under fiscal year 2027,

**[03:38:27.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003140)  
how does the PSC ensure that this inclusive sports program

**[03:38:33.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003141)  
receive adequate and predictable funding,

**[03:38:36.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003142)  
and what specific amounts are programmed for them for fiscal year 2027?

**[03:38:44.360] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003143)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor,

**[03:38:45.540] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003144)  
while regular appropriations for national sports for all grassroots centerpiece programs

**[03:38:51.800] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003145)  
stand at

**[03:38:52.920] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003146)  
63.48 million in the fiscal year 2027,

**[03:38:57.280] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003147)  
down from 80.07 million in 2026,

**[03:39:03.860] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003148)  
the PSC supplements regular line items

**[03:39:07.280] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003149)  
by drawing from the off-budget National Sports Development Fund

**[03:39:11.140] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003150)  
under Section 26 of RA 6847,

**[03:39:14.480] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003151)  
automatic remittances from Pagcor gross income shares

**[03:39:18.560] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003152)  
and PCS or sweepstakes proceeds

**[03:39:20.780] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003153)  
are legally earmarked to finance,

**[03:39:22.920] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003154)  
multi-level sports competition,

**[03:39:26.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003155)  
and inclusive community projects like women in sports,

**[03:39:31.420] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003156)  
indigenous people's games and Pilipinas para games.

**[03:39:36.060] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003157)  
Your Honor.

**[03:39:37.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003158)  
Mr. Speaker, that will end my interpellation.

**[03:39:42.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003159)  
Thank you very much.

**[03:39:43.860] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003160)  
Thank you.

**[03:39:44.860] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003161)  
Before that, good sponsor,

**[03:39:46.860] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003162)  
I just would like to do a few clarification.

**[03:39:49.860] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003163)  
Does the Philippine Sports Commission consider,

**[03:39:52.920] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003164)  
tying up with renowned academy

**[03:39:57.920] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003165)  
for putting them here in the Philippines

**[03:39:59.920] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003166)  
so that the training, technology,

**[03:40:02.920] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003167)

**[03:40:11.150] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003168)  
Ginagawa pa po natin 'yon?

**[03:40:12.410] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003169)  
Yes, we are very interested and we are pursuing that, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:40:21.350] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003170)  
Okay, good to know.

**[03:40:22.080] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003171)  
Kasi lahat ng mga

**[03:40:24.000] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003172)  
sikat natin na athletes talagang nag-train abroad eh.

**[03:40:27.359] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003173)  
Tapos doon sila nakakakuha ng mga, uh, well, skills na

**[03:40:32.720] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003174)  
nagiging, uh, very successful ang kanilang career.

**[03:40:36.680] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003175)  
No, so Majority Leader.

**[03:40:42.160] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-003176)  
Mr. Speaker, next to interpellate the sponsor,

**[03:40:45.160] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-003177)  
I move to recognize Representative Jet Nisay

**[03:40:48.160] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-003178)  
from Pusong Pinoy Party List.

**[03:40:52.160] Deputy Speaker Alfredo "Albee" B. Benitez** (asr-003179)  
Representative from Pusong Pinoy Party List is recognized.

**[03:40:56.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003180)  
Good morning, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[03:41:00.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003181)  
A few questions lamang po para po sa PSC.

**[03:41:04.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003182)  
Unang-una ho, pagdating sa grassroots po natin.

**[03:41:09.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003183)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, sa Batang Pinoy na umaabot

**[03:41:14.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003184)  
sa around 15,000 young athletes aged 17 and below

**[03:41:18.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003185)  
na nagko-compete sa 27 sports.

**[03:41:21.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003186)  
Paano po natin sinusundan ang kanilang development

**[03:41:25.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003187)  
pagkatapos nilang makilahok?

**[03:41:27.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003188)  
At ilan sa kanilang eventually napupunta sa national training pool?

**[03:41:45.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003189)  
After, Mr. Speaker, Your Honor,

**[03:41:47.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003190)  
after Batang Pinoy po,

**[03:41:49.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003191)  
kasunod po ang Palarong Pambansa sa pag-monitor sa ating mga athletes.

**[03:42:05.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003192)  
Then ang NSA po ang nagsa-check ng mga best performances sa ating mga athletes.

**[03:42:13.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003193)  
At ang PSC po ay merong youth pool.

**[03:42:17.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003194)  
Your Honor.

**[03:42:21.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003195)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[03:42:23.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003196)  
napakalaga po nito dahil hindi nagtatapos sa isang torneo ang talent identification.

**[03:42:29.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003197)  
Dapat ay may sistema para masundan at ma-develop ang mga atleta na may angking husay at talento.

**[03:42:35.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003198)  
Ang follow-up question ko lamang ho, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[03:42:39.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003199)  
may centralized talent tracking system po ba ang PSC?

**[03:42:43.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003200)  
From grassroots happening,

**[03:42:45.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003201)  
elite level?

**[03:42:47.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003202)  
At may data po ba kung ilan sa mga dating palarumpang bansa at Batang Pinoy athletes ang umabot na sa national level o team?

**[03:43:01.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003203)  
Yes po, meron pong tracking yan.

**[03:43:05.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003204)  
Ang humahawak po niyan ay ang NSAs.

**[03:43:07.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003205)  
At ang mga kanya-kanyang

**[03:43:09.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003206)  
ahm

**[03:43:11.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003207)  
ahm

**[03:43:13.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003208)  
Sanasabit po sa PSC ang mga list ng mga atleta?

**[03:43:23.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003209)  
Thank you Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[03:43:27.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003210)  
Ang pangalawang katanungan ko po,

**[03:43:29.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003211)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[03:43:31.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003212)  
May 44 million plus po sa NEP, sa 27 NEP para sa regional training centers.

**[03:43:39.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003213)  
Ilang centers po ang target nitong ponduhan

**[03:43:41.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003214)  
at saan po sila ilalagay at ilang atlets ang direktang makikinabang.

**[03:44:01.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003215)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, PSC will support 44, 40 sports

**[03:44:11.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003216)  
focused on Olympic sports.

**[03:44:16.820] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003217)  
At kada sport po, merong tatlong regional training center

**[03:44:25.460] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003218)  
in Luzon, Visayas, and Mindanao.

**[03:44:29.460] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003219)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, napakalaga po nito

**[03:44:32.260] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003220)  
dahil hindi lahat ng promising atlets ay malapit sa Metro Manila.

**[03:44:37.460] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003221)  
Kung kulang ang regional training infrastructure,

**[03:44:39.580] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003222)  
mas mahirap po.

**[03:44:40.920] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003223)  
Sa atlets from the provinces na magkaroon

**[03:44:43.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003224)  
ng regular access sa quality training and support.

**[03:44:47.320] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003225)  
Ang follow-up question ko po,

**[03:44:48.920] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003226)  
ano po ang kriteriya ng PSC

**[03:44:50.540] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003227)  
sa pagpili ng regions na magkakaroon ng regional training centers?

**[03:44:55.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003228)  
Nakabatay rin ba ito kung nag-excel ang kada region o probinsya?

**[03:45:00.000] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003229)  
Yes, totoo po yan.

**[03:45:06.140] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003230)  
Since Chairman Gregorio took office in July 2025, PSC has established at least 21 locations, project nationwide, covering completed and ongoing centers.

**[03:45:21.200] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003231)  
These efforts are urgently being implemented in response to express directive from the Office of the President.

**[03:45:27.980] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003232)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, may national assessment po ba ang PSC kung ating regions ang underserved in terms of sports facilities and training capacity?

**[03:45:50.920] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003233)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, si Chairman po ang mismong umiikot sa...

**[03:45:57.980] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003234)  
...bansa natin para pag-inspect at pagtingin sa mga sitwasyon ng kada probinsya.

**[03:46:04.200] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003235)  
Your Honor.

**[03:46:07.060] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003236)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, mas maganda po siguro mas madagdagan pa o mas lakihan pa yung pondo pagdating po sa mga sports center po natin across sa 18 regions po natin.

**[03:46:21.900] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003237)  
So yung 44 million plus po na to, kulang na kulang.

**[03:46:27.980] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003238)  
Hindi po doblihin, triplihin o mas lakihan pa.

**[03:46:31.040] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003239)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[03:46:34.260] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003240)

**[03:46:44.840] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003241)  
nagbawas po ng 40.57% amounting to 895,364,000.

**[03:46:51.820] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003242)  
Pero alam ko po sa ating bagong chairman ng Philippine Sports Commission,

**[03:46:57.980] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003243)  
po yung kanyang ginagawa at mga achievements, kahit maiksi pa lang ang kanyang panunungkulan,

**[03:47:03.760] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003244)  
e marami na pong mga nagawa ng mga regional training centers.

**[03:47:08.460] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003245)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[03:47:12.580] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003246)  
Pagdating naman po sa sports as an economic industry,

**[03:47:16.700] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003247)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[03:47:19.360] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003248)  
ang sports is not only government expenditure,

**[03:47:22.360] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003249)

**[03:47:27.980] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003250)  
medicine, fitness, events, broadcasting, tourism facilities, and sports equipment.

**[03:47:35.760] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003251)  
May national strategy po ba ang PSC para mapalagong sports economy?

**[03:47:49.000] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003252)  
Sa totoo lang po, base kay chairman, dyan po ang talagang isa sa mandato ng PSC coming from the President.

**[03:47:57.960] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003253)  
President himself, Administrative Order No. 38,

**[03:48:06.060] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003254)  
creating the Sports Tourism Council to support economic development, Your Honor.

**[03:48:17.940] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003255)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[03:48:20.360] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003256)  
Ang aking follow-up question, nakikipag-coordinate po ba ang PSC with DTI and DOT upang gawing economic activity?

**[03:48:27.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003257)  
In major sporting events and sports tourism.

**[03:48:42.940] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003258)  
Ang DOT po, Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, ang DOT po ang tumatayong vice chairman ng NST committee at ang chairman po ang PSC.

**[03:48:57.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003259)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, may estimate din po ba ang PSC kung ilang jobs and economic activity ang nililika ng major sporting events and sports facilities?

**[03:49:27.960] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003261)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, sa economic indicator po natin, sa $1 na gastos sa bawat sports tourism, ang balik po niyan ay times 15.

**[03:49:54.940] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003262)  
According to the Nielsen Report, Your Honor.

**[03:49:57.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003263)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[03:50:02.020] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003264)  
Pagdating naman po sa ating athlete safety, lessons from the Baterbonia and Adili tragedy,

**[03:50:09.860] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003265)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, kamakailan lamang po ay nasawi ang ating dalawang student-athletes na si Rene Baterbonia at Divine Adili sa isang team-building activity.

**[03:50:20.780] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003266)  
Nagpanukala rin po ang inyong lingkod ng House Bill 9892 o ang Local of Campus Activities Safety Act.

**[03:50:27.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003267)  
Na layong magkaroon ng safety standards at risk classification ang iba't-ibang off-campus activities ng mga mag-aaral sa ilalim ng DepEd, CHED, TESDA.

**[03:50:37.840] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003268)  
Pero para sa Philippines Sports Commission na may mandato sa athletes welfare at sports development, ano pong specific role ng PSC sa pagtiyak ng kaligtasan ng ating mga atleta, lalo na kapag sila ay kabilang sa school-based sports programs at officially sanctioned activities?

**[03:50:57.960] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003269)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, nagtatag na po ang PSC ng Stakeholders Committee together with DepEd, CHED, UAAP, SBP, and National Youth Commission.

**[03:51:22.160] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003270)  
At nabanggit siya po yung nangyaring disgrasya sa...

**[03:51:27.960] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003271)  
Sa Ateneo, PSC extended P250,000 to each family.

**[03:51:34.720] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003272)  
Your Honor.

**[03:51:36.340] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003273)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[03:51:39.880] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003274)  
Follow-up question ko lamang po.

**[03:51:41.620] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003275)  
Dahil marami pong student-athletes ay mula sa private schools, malinaw po ba kung hanggang saan ng responsibilidad ng PSC, ng school, ng National Sports Association, at ng event organizer kapag may serious injury o fatal accident sa isang sports-related activities?

**[03:51:57.960] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003276)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, sa mga ganyang sitwasyon po, ang nangunguna po dyan ay ang CHED,

**[03:52:25.220] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003277)  
at ang...

**[03:52:27.960] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003278)  
sponsoring league, for example, UAAP, and then...

**[03:52:38.720] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003279)  
PSC is developing a National Athlete and Coaches Code with strong iterations of safe sport principles.

**[03:52:48.180] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003280)  
It is also backing stronger coaching and safeguarding standards, including the proposed Sports Coaching Act, House Bill 2631,

**[03:52:57.000] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003281)  
which...

**[03:52:57.300] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003282)  
which seeks a national multi-sport coaching accreditation system, a national roster of accredited coaches, continuing education, and stronger safety and competency requirements.

**[03:53:12.520] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003283)  
Your Honor.

**[03:53:13.360] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003284)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[03:53:15.980] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003285)  
Bilang huling katalungan na lamang po, familiar po ba kayo sa mga tinatawag na pay-to-play in sports pagdating po sa grassroots program po natin?

**[03:53:27.300] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003286)

**[03:53:43.240] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003287)  
Oo.

**[03:53:44.120] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003288)

**[03:53:51.620] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003289)  
pay-to-play sa grassroots and youth sports.

**[03:53:54.280] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003290)  
Alam po ninyo, marami pong mga organization.

**[03:53:57.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003291)  
Ang bumababa sa mga probinsya.

**[03:53:59.520] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003292)  
So, ito po yung mga sports organization na hindi ko po alam kung nakikipag-partner po ba ito sa atin pong Philippine Sports Commission.

**[03:54:07.220] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003293)  
Ang nangyayari, malalaking liga, maglalaro ang mga bata, pero kailangan nilang gumastos para sa pang-sarili nilang mga entrance fee, mga uniform, iba't iba pong mga gastos pagdating sa sports program.

**[03:54:24.780] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003294)  
Ano po kaya yung maaaring tulong?

**[03:54:27.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003295)

**[03:54:37.640] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003296)  
Mr. Speaker, Your Honor, yung po bang sinasabi nyo, yung mga nakikita nating mga ligang labas?

**[03:54:44.420] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003297)

**[03:54:51.520] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003298)  
Pwedeng basketball, pwedeng volleyball, pwedeng football.

**[03:54:57.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003299)

**[03:55:13.740] Rep. Gerardo J. Espina Jr.** (asr-003300)

**[03:55:24.620] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003301)

**[03:55:40.560] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003302)

**[03:55:54.620] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003303)  
At, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, I am manifesting my full support para sa Philippine Sports Commission.

**[03:56:02.100] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003304)

**[03:56:34.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003308)

**[04:31:03.500] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003904)  
Session is resumed. Majority Leader?

**[04:31:06.640] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003904-split-2)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to resume consideration of House Bill No. 10858.

**[04:31:12.180] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003904-split-3)  
Is there any objection? Chair hears none. Motion is approved. Majority Leader?

**[04:31:16.740] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003904-split-4)  
Mr. Speaker, the parliamentary status of House Bill No. 10858 is that it is in the period of sponsorship and debate. I now move to consider the proposed budget of the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development.

**[04:31:30.500] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003905)  
Is there any objection? Chair hears none. Motion is approved. Majority Leader?

**[04:31:37.140] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003905-split-2)  
Mr. Speaker, may we acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials from the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development, namely Secretary-Engineer Jose Ramon P. Aliling, Undersecretary Henry L. Yap, Undersecretary Eduardo P. Robles Jr., Undersecretary Atty. Marisol D. Anenias,

**[04:32:00.220] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003906)  
Undersecretary Richard Michael N. Dimagiba, Undersecretary Atty. Roman Nicolo S. Molina, Executive Commissioner Atty. Avelino D. Tolentino III, General Manager Joeben A. Tai, President and CEO Federico A. Laxa, President Renato L. Tobias from the National Home Mortgage Finance Corporation, and Chief Executive Officer Marilyn C. Acosta from the Home Development Mutual Fund and Pag-IBIG.

**[04:32:33.220] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003907)  
We acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials from the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development, including attached agencies and corporations, headed by Secretary Aliling. Welcome to the House of Representatives.

**[04:32:49.020] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003907-split-2)  
Majority Leader?

**[04:32:50.700] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003907-split-3)  
Mr. Speaker, I now move to recognize Representative Julienne Jam Baronda, Chairperson and Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations, to

**[04:33:00.000] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003908)  
To answer questions on the proposed budget of the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development.

**[04:33:06.080] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003908-split-3)  
Representative Jam Baronda is recognized. Please proceed.

**[04:33:19.700] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003908-split-2)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished members of this honorable chamber, fellow public servants and partners in public service

**[04:33:30.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003909)  
from the housing sector headed by Secretary Jose Ramon 'Ping' Aliling. Before I proceed, allow me to share an important development in our assessment of the country's housing needs. The country's housing need is not 6.9 million units but

**[04:33:59.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003910)  
only 3.77 million units, as established by the Philippine Statistics Authority's improved methodology. The new framework provides a more precise assessment of actual housing conditions and the different ways these needs may be addressed.

**[04:34:20.200] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003911)  
The earlier estimates included households building or repairing their own homes, doubled-up households or families living with another family, newly formed households, and projected housing losses due to calamities.

**[04:34:40.200] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003912)  
The refined methodology takes into account the capacity of some families to undertake repairs, rehabilitation or home construction, resulting in a more targeted estimate of 3.76 million housing units.

**[04:35:05.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003913)  
This figure should not be viewed as a requirement for the government to build 3.7 million new houses. Addressing housing needs requires both direct and indirect interventions, including affordable housing production, community development, home improvement and accessible financing.

**[04:35:34.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003914)  
The Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development, under the expanded Pambansang Pabahay para sa Pilipino or 4PH program, is implementing indirect housing programs such as the Enhanced Community Mortgage Program or ECMP and land awards under presidential proclamations.

**[04:36:00.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003915)  
These efforts address housing needs through secure land tenure and community development without requiring construction of a new housing unit for every family. Equally important is the balance between supply and demand.

**[04:36:20.200] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003916)  
Developers provide supply while families and individuals create demand. Developers will only build housing if there are buyers who can afford to purchase homes. This is where Pag-IBIG Fund financing becomes crucial.

**[04:36:41.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003917)  
Financing now covers both socialized and economic housing, with loan amounts up to 10 million pesos. More families are given the capacity to purchase homes. In turn, stronger demand encourages developers to produce more housing, creating a sustainable cycle of supply and demand. Through the expanded 4PH program, together with Pag-IBIG Fund, local government units, private developers and communities, the government can address housing needs through a combination of housing production, indirect interventions and accessible financing, bringing safe, decent and affordable homes within reach for more Filipino families.

**[04:37:49.660] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003918)  
Last year, during the fiscal year 2026 budget deliberation, DHSUD and its key shelter agencies presented the recalibrated and expanded 4PH program. Today, we are pleased to report that the program has gone beyond planning and policy reform into actual housing delivery and contributes significantly to the more than 500,000 housing units delivered, reported by the President in his SONA.

**[04:38:30.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003919)  
Last July. Guided by the vision of President Ferdinand R. Marcos Jr. and pursuant to Executive Order No. 34, the housing sector adopted a more inclusive, beneficiary-centered and multimodal housing strategy, recognizing that no single housing solution can address the diverse needs of Filipino families.

**[04:39:00.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003920)  
In one year of implementation, the expanded 4PH program has transformed from a vertical housing initiative into a comprehensive and inclusive housing platform. It now includes horizontal development and the Enhanced Community Mortgage Program.

**[04:39:30.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003921)  
It also includes residential proclamation sites, rental housing, modular shelter units, high-density housing, Pag-IBIG Fund and ROPA financing assistance modalities. As of August 2026, the expanded 4PH program generated 135,758 housing units.

**[04:40:00.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003922)  
across its various modalities. This represent a significant increase from 1,024 units produced during the first 35 months of the original 4PH program, demonstrating the impact of the reforms and expansion undertaken by the department. These are not merely housing units in the pipeline.

**[04:40:30.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003923)  
Families are already moving into completed communities. The program has completed 15,543 vertical housing units and 15,775 horizontal housing units, with thousands of families occupying their homes.

**[04:41:00.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003924)  
Access to affordable financing has expanded. As of September 7, 2026, more than 11,700 beneficiary families have availed of housing units under the program, over 70% from the lowest income deciles.

**[04:41:30.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003925)  
Beyond direct housing production, the department continues to strengthen long-term, sustainable housing delivery through policy, regulatory and institutional reforms. These reforms strengthen the enabling environment for housing production.

**[04:42:00.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003926)  
and private sector partners can deliver more housing efficiently and at a greater scale.

**[04:42:10.380] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003927)  
These efforts contribute to the broader housing agenda of this administration,

**[04:42:16.120] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003928)  
which aims to facilitate the production, financing, and provision of 1.13 million housing units

**[04:42:27.260] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003929)  
by the end of June 2028.

**[04:42:31.860] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003930)  
With the expanded 4PH program contributing one in every four of these housing units,

**[04:42:39.880] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003931)  
to sustain this momentum, the Department respectfully proposed a fiscal year 2027 budget

**[04:42:49.080] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003932)  
of approximately 5.88 billion, including 1.1 billion

**[04:42:57.260] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003934)  
for housing interest subsidies and program administration and implementation of the expanded 4PH program.

**[04:43:07.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003935)  
On this note, Mr. Speaker, we thank the Committee on Appropriation and the Committee of Housing

**[04:43:13.360] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003936)  
and Urban Development for their support and consideration of the Department's budget proposal.

**[04:43:20.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003937)  
We likewise respectfully seek the support of this Honorable Committee

**[04:43:26.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003939)  
In restoring and strengthening funding for crucial housing intervention necessary the gains of expanded 4-page program

**[04:43:35.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003940)  
and achieve the housing targets of the administration.

**[04:43:40.180] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003941)  
This proposed funding will allow the government to continue making housing affordable,

**[04:43:46.680] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003942)  
expanded access to subsidized financing, support vulnerable families, strengthen project implementation,

**[04:43:56.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003943)  
and accelerate the delivery of housing units nationwide.

**[04:44:02.540] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003944)  
Ultimately, Mr. Speaker, the success of our housing program is not measured solely

**[04:44:09.840] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003945)  
by budgets obligated,

**[04:44:12.760] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003946)  
projects launched,

**[04:44:14.840] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003947)  
or units completed.

**[04:44:17.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003948)  
It is measured by the number of Filipino families whose lives are transformed

**[04:44:24.680] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003949)  
because they finally

**[04:44:26.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003950)  
have a safe, secure, and a dignified place to call home.

**[04:44:34.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003951)  
With the continued support of this Congress,

**[04:44:38.180] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003952)  
our partner agencies, local government units,

**[04:44:42.500] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003953)  
financial institutions, developers, and communities,

**[04:44:46.940] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003954)  
we remain committed to building more homes,

**[04:44:51.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003955)  
strengthening communities,

**[04:44:53.480] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003956)  
and ensuring that more Filipino families can proudly say,

**[04:45:00.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003957)  
May sariling tahanan na kami. Hay, salamat siya, Diyos. May balay na gid ako.

**[04:45:07.420] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003958)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:45:09.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003959)  
This representation, Mr. Speaker, once again requests this august body to consider the proposed budget of the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development,

**[04:45:21.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003960)  
National Housing Authority, Social Housing Finance Corporation, and National Home Mortgage Financing Corporation for the consideration of this honorable body.

**[04:45:35.440] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003961)  
Thank you very much. Majority Leader?

**[04:45:38.000] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003962)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to recognize Representative Iris Marie Demesa Montes of 4K Party-list to interpellate the sponsor on the proposed budget of the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development.

**[04:45:51.420] Deputy Speaker Vincent Franco "Duke" D. Frasco** (asr-003964)  
Representative Montes is recognized to interpellate the sponsor. You have 30 minutes.

**[04:45:57.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003964-split-2)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. Magandang hapon po, distinguished colleagues at mga kasama sa DHSUD.

**[04:46:04.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003965)  
Salamat po sa ating sponsor sa kanyang sponsorship speech sa budget po ng D-SUDE.

**[04:46:12.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003966)  
Napakarami pong impormasyon, napakaraming numero.

**[04:46:16.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003967)  
So, gusto ko po sanang maunawaan, isimplify, maintindihan po yung mga sinabi ninyo.

**[04:46:24.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003968)  
So, please allow me, kung maaari po, umpisahan natin ang aking mga katanungan.

**[04:46:32.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003969)  
Una po, ano po ang actual housing need po natin?

**[04:46:37.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003970)  
Let's start po. Ano po ang kailangan, numero ng pabahay na kailangan natin sa ngayon?

**[04:46:46.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003971)  
Based on 24, on this date is 3.77 million po, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:46:57.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003972)  
So, yan po ang pangangailangan. Ano naman po ang backlog natin sa ngayon?

**[04:47:06.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003973)  
Ang backlog po natin ngayon, as of this date, is 2.2 million.

**[04:47:11.640] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003974)  
2.2 million.

**[04:47:13.500] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003975)  
Ano naman po ang actual na na-construct na natin sa ngayon, as we speak?

**[04:47:21.800] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003976)  
Ang sinabi ko po lahat, based on the recent, 136,000.

**[04:47:32.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003977)  
136,000.

**[04:47:33.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003978)  
Sa iba't-ibang...

**[04:47:34.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003979)  
Yes po, housing units.

**[04:47:36.060] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003980)  
Housing units.

**[04:47:36.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003981)  
Out of the 136,000 na na-construct po, I suppose these are ready for occupancy.

**[04:47:43.500] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003982)  
Tama po?

**[04:47:44.320] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003983)  
Yes po.

**[04:47:45.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003984)  
Out of this po, ilan naman po dito ang actual na po na-ocupa?

**[04:47:52.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003985)  
I mean, na-turnover na po natin out of the 136,000?

**[04:47:59.920] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003987)  
Based po sa...

**[04:48:07.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003988)  
11,000...

**[04:48:13.500] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003989)  
700 beneficiary families has successfully availed dowsing units under this program.

**[04:48:21.560] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003990)  
So...

**[04:48:21.940] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003991)  
So, kasi, Madam, Mr. Speaker, yung iba kasi CMP.

**[04:48:26.640] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003992)  
So, ito yung occupants, yung bahay na sinasabi, or units, ito po yun.

**[04:48:32.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003993)  
Okay.

**[04:48:32.940] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003994)  
Pero with CMP, with the mortgage out of the program, 136,000 po lahat, beneficiaries, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:48:43.500] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003995)  
So, kung ipuporsyento po natin, ano, out of the current 2.2 backlog, we have actually constructed about 136,000 housing units.

**[04:48:57.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003996)  
So, that's just about ilang porsyento po ito.

**[04:49:02.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-003997)  
Wala pa po ito sa 10%, roughly 6%, am I correct?

**[04:49:08.580] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003999)  
Actually, Mr. Speaker, okay.

**[04:49:11.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004000)  
Kasi this is under the new modality.

**[04:49:13.500] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004001)  
Dati na 4-page program, iba yun.

**[04:49:17.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004002)  
It's impossible to do.

**[04:49:19.860] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004003)  
Kaya po ginagawa natin because we have to also consider yung naka-pipeline pa.

**[05:09:21.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004329)

**[05:09:25.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004330)

**[05:09:37.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004331)
