**[05:24:36.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004570)  
So, in short, mayroon pala kayo rental housing program. May program pala ito.

**[05:24:42.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004571)  
Mr. Speaker, for rental housing po, sa government nyo, and then after matapos,

**[05:24:48.260] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004572)  
i-turnover na po sa local government units, sila na po nag-administer.

**[05:24:54.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004573)  
So, ang LGU, once being done, transferred to LGU, sila na mag-maintain?

**[05:24:59.800] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004574)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:25:01.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004575)  
And then, even the ownership ba noon, o hindi lang yung ano lang?

**[05:25:05.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004576)  
Yung management lang?

**[05:25:06.140] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004577)  
Rental lang po, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:25:07.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004578)  
Okay.

**[05:25:07.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004579)  
So, what are the eligibility requirements, and how does the SHUD determine,

**[05:25:17.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004580)  
and validate whether a household actually requires subsidized rental housing?

**[05:25:25.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004581)  
Actually, Mr. Speaker, ang priority natin dyan is yung mga nasa danger zones,

**[05:25:31.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004582)  
and yung LGU din po nag-identify.

**[05:25:34.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004583)  
So, the LGU is the one...

**[05:25:35.960] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004584)  
Danger zones, tapos yung mga napektuhan ng programa, proyekto ng gobyerno, yun po.

**[05:25:41.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004585)  
So, what are the mechanics of the subsidy?

**[05:25:47.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004586)  
How much of the rent is shouldered by the government?

**[05:25:51.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004587)  
How much is paid by the bilipasyari?

**[05:25:54.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004588)  
And for how long can a household receive assistance?

**[05:25:59.100] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004589)  
Mr. Speaker, yung binabayad po nila, rental fee, sobrang mababa na po yun.

**[05:26:05.100] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004590)  
Para lang po yun for the estate management.

**[05:26:08.340] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004591)  
So, definitely po walang kita ang gobyerno natin dyan.

**[05:26:14.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004592)  
Actually, hindi ko sinabi yung mga committee gobyerno.

**[05:26:17.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004593)  
So, how much...

**[05:26:17.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004594)  
How much rent shouldered by the government?

**[05:26:22.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004595)  
Sila ba may share sila sa pagbayad o wala?

**[05:26:25.580] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004596)  
Mr. Speaker, yung sinolder po ng government, yung pagawa mismo ng building.

**[05:26:32.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004597)  
So, what is the process for a family to apply for and avail itself of the program?

**[05:26:38.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004598)  
Ito ba available po sa lahat ng Pilipino or there should be...

**[05:26:42.040] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004599)  
Actually, dati po.

**[05:26:43.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004600)  
In LGU kasi na po ang merong...

**[05:26:47.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004601)  
Kahit sino?

**[05:26:47.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004602)  
Knowledge po niyan.

**[05:26:48.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004603)  
Kahit sino, pwede mag-apply o yung mga nasa lay-in muna.

**[05:26:51.300] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004604)  
Meron po tayo mechanics. Meron po mechanics ang LGU.

**[05:26:54.580] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004605)  
Una is, yun nga, yun na sa danger zone.

**[05:26:57.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004606)  
Second, yung ma-informal settlers na na-evict sa mga pamamahay nila.

**[05:27:02.680] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004607)  
Third, yung mga nasalanta ng magyo, yung mga dilubyo, yung nasunugan.

**[05:27:08.340] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004608)  
Like, best example po, dito sa NIA.

**[05:27:11.020] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004609)  
Nasunog yung babae nila. Ngayon, ginagawa po ng gobyerno.

**[05:27:14.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004610)  
Sila din yung po yung titira, yung mga biktima po ng sunog.

**[05:27:17.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004611)  
Fair enough. Good answer.

**[05:27:21.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004612)  
Thank you.

**[05:27:22.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004613)  
As of today, how many rental housing units have actually been completed and made available for occupancy?

**[05:27:30.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004614)  
And how many families are currently renting units under the program?

**[05:27:36.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004615)  
Mr. Speaker, almost 5,000 po. Ginagawa pa lang ngayon.

**[05:27:39.960] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004616)  
But next year, first quarter, mag-start na po ng turnover.

**[05:27:45.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004617)  
Ng 5,000 units?

**[05:27:46.360] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004618)  
Yes po.

**[05:27:46.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004619)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:27:47.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004620)  
Are you sure?

**[05:27:48.680] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004621)  
Yes po. Start pa lang.

**[05:27:50.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004622)  
How can you be sure?

**[05:27:51.340] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004623)  
I can be sure because I have it in my district.

**[05:27:53.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004624)  
Okay. Thank you.

**[05:27:56.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004625)  
Now, my follow-up question.

**[05:27:58.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004626)  
The Social Housing Finance Corporation continues to implement the Community Mortgage Program

**[05:28:04.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004627)  
which assists organized communities in acquiring and developing land for housing.

**[05:28:11.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004628)  
Some Community Mortgage Program projects have also been presented as part of

**[05:28:17.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004629)  
the accomplishment of the 4-Page Program.

**[05:28:20.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004630)  
Question. What criteria does the SHFC use in determining which communities or sites will be prioritized for financing under the Community Mortgage Program?

**[05:28:35.040] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004631)  
Mr. Speaker, sa totoo lang po, pag may lupa na available, pag-organize si Homeowners Association, magpapadala po ng member ng board,

**[05:28:47.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004632)  
si SHFC, tapos first-come, first-served sa makakumplay.

**[05:28:54.840] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004633)  
So, wala pong problema po sa beneficiary, basta po, kompleto, in compliance po sa mga requirement.

**[05:29:02.960] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004634)  
Best example, for example, si LGU or si DSUD, meron pong available ang lot na i-offer ang isang private individual.

**[05:29:15.760] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004635)  
Pag nagkakasundo po sa presyo, posible po na magbibigyan ng importansya ni SHFC.

**[05:29:24.700] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004636)  
What is good with this, meron po silang dalawang team.

**[05:29:27.740] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004637)  
Una, yung appraisal. Second, yung member po ng board or yung komposisyon po ng engineer, architects.

**[05:29:35.320] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004638)  
So, they would check the area. Then, after po dyan, if necessary na mag-meet sila, often in a week, gagawin po nila yan.

**[05:29:45.760] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004639)  
Kaya po, importante talaga for LGU, for local government units sa area na sila mismo tutulungan po yung hoa nila makumplay yung mga dokumento na needed po.

**[05:29:57.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004640)  
Thank you.

**[05:29:57.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004641)  
For Community Mortgage Program Project,

**[05:30:00.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004642)  
being reported as 4PH accomplishments.

**[05:30:03.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004643)  
How many are genuinely new projects initiated under 4PH

**[05:30:07.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004644)  
and how many had already been identified,

**[05:30:12.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004645)  
organized or processed before they were included under the 4PH banner?

**[05:30:17.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004646)  
47, Mr. Speaker, around 8,000 families po.

**[05:30:23.080] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004647)  
And meron pa po yung naka-next in line sa pipeline po for this year.

**[05:30:27.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004648)  
So next in line meron pa?

**[05:30:28.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004649)  
Yes po.

**[05:30:29.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004650)  
And how, ilan sila?

**[05:30:31.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004651)  
Another 40.

**[05:30:32.880] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004652)  
Pero Mr. Speaker, sa totoo lang, ang challenge po dun sa WHOAS

**[05:30:36.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004653)  
is hindi po nila kaya i-organize yung mga members nila.

**[05:30:41.740] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004654)  
But that is why, kaya po si D. Sud nandoon na rin

**[05:30:45.100] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004655)  
para tutulungan din yung local members nila.

**[05:30:48.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004656)  
Thank you.

**[05:30:49.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004657)  
For projects that were already in the SHFC pipeline before 4PH,

**[05:30:57.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004658)  
what additional intervention or financing

**[05:30:59.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004659)  
was provided under 4PH that warrants counting them

**[05:31:04.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004660)  
as 4PH accomplishments?

**[05:31:06.560] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004661)  
Actually po, Mr. Speaker, sa enhanced mortgage program,

**[05:31:12.220] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004662)  
sinasama, allowed na po, at sinasama na nga yung utilities,

**[05:31:15.820] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004663)  
yung land developments, streetlights po,

**[05:31:17.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004664)  
yan po yung bagong programa ngayon ng SHFC.

**[05:31:20.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004665)  
So, yung mga lahat yan?

**[05:31:23.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004666)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:31:24.820] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004667)  
Okay.

**[05:31:27.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004668)  
How were these...

**[05:31:29.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004669)  
sites prioritized?

**[05:31:31.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004670)  
Particularly when the acquisition of the property is financed

**[05:31:34.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004671)  
using SHFC escrow funds?

**[05:31:38.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004672)  
So, paano nyo i-prioritize yung location?

**[05:31:42.200] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004673)  
Mr. Speaker, as what I've said po, Mr. Speaker,

**[05:31:47.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004674)  
kung sino ang una makapag-comply ng dokumento?

**[05:31:53.480] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004675)  
Kasi una, marami din request sa buong Pilipinas.

**[05:31:57.280] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004676)  
Especially po, ako, tip ko sa lahat,

**[05:31:59.480] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004677)  
dapat mura para i-prioritize siya.

**[05:32:03.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004678)  
Kasi there are areas na sobrang mahal na.

**[05:32:07.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004679)  
Unaffordable na yung cost per square meters.

**[05:32:12.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004680)  
Okay.

**[05:32:12.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004681)  
Follow-up question.

**[05:32:13.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004682)  
Can SHFC provide a breakdown of the community mortgage program projects

**[05:32:19.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004683)  
currently counted under 4PH,

**[05:32:22.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004684)  
indicating when each project was first identified,

**[05:32:25.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004685)  
when it entered the SHFC pipeline,

**[05:32:29.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004686)  
when it was included under 4PH,

**[05:32:32.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004687)  
the number of beneficiaries and the source of financing?

**[05:32:38.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004688)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:32:39.420] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004689)  
We will comply.

**[05:32:40.200] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004690)  
We will submit.

**[05:32:41.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004691)  
Okay.

**[05:32:45.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004692)  
Ito, very interesting to.

**[05:32:47.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004693)  
During Secretary Jose Ramon Aliling's confirmation proceedings,

**[05:32:52.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004694)  
then Congressman Luis Raymond L. Ray Villapuerta Jr.

**[05:32:56.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004695)  
raised the issue of the Secretary's interest in private property.

**[05:32:59.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004696)  
He also raised the issue of the Secretary's interest in private companies

**[05:33:00.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004697)  
and whether this interest will be divested upon assuming public office.

**[05:33:05.040] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004698)  
Ang problema natin doon sa Secretary natin, single pa kasi eh.

**[05:33:08.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004699)  
Kaya walang puro trabaho.

**[05:33:10.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004700)  
Pero ngayon, I can assure you, 100% fully divested na po yung properties

**[05:33:14.960] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004701)  
at yung mga interest ni Mr. Secretary sa corporation.

**[05:33:19.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004702)  
I have nothing against the Secretary.

**[05:33:21.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004703)  
I just saw it during the confirmation, so I have to relay lang ha.

**[05:33:25.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004704)  
I have nothing to do with this.

**[05:33:26.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004705)  
Because, you know, since this is a budget, ano,

**[05:33:29.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004706)  
okay, so we need to discuss everything.

**[05:33:35.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004707)  
Secretary, may we ask whether you have already completed,

**[05:33:38.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004708)  
again, you already answered that, no?

**[05:33:42.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004709)  
If yes, when was the divestment completed?

**[05:33:47.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004710)  
And has the appropriate documentation been submitted to the relevant government agencies?

**[05:33:52.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004711)  
I guess you complied that already.

**[05:33:54.940] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004712)  
Mr. Speaker, according to the good Secretary,

**[05:33:56.960] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004713)  
as for the divestment,

**[05:33:59.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004714)  
do you presently retain any direct or indirect financial interest

**[05:34:06.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004715)  
in any company engaged in construction, real estate, development, engineering, property?

**[05:34:12.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004716)  
According to the good Secretary, 100% divested.

**[05:34:15.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004717)  
Hindi pa ako tapos. Galing mo sa umugot, ha?

**[05:34:19.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004718)  
Given the technical and highly specialized nature of housing policy,

**[05:34:24.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004719)  
urban development, housing finance, estate management,

**[05:34:28.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004720)  
and so on,

**[05:34:29.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004721)  
the development planning, the professional expertise of the SHUD's senior leadership

**[05:34:34.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004722)  
is important to the effective implementation of the department's programs.

**[05:34:40.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004723)  
Among the department's current undersecretaries and assistant secretaries

**[05:34:45.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004724)  
who have professional or technical backgrounds specifically in housing and urban development.

**[05:34:52.300] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004725)  
Mr. Secretary, sa totoo, ah, Mr. Speaker, sa totoo lang,

**[05:34:55.700] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004726)  
kaya nawawalan RD sa buong Pilipinas, kasi iyon ang ginawa niya yung UCI.

**[05:34:59.300] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004727)  
Yusek, ASEC, so all of, sa may mga experience,

**[05:35:03.140] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004728)  
si Yusek Henry, galing po yan sa developer po yan.

**[05:35:07.920] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004729)  
Oo, kaya pagkausap mo, alam na nila lahat ng mga solusyon.

**[05:35:12.220] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004730)  
Ako may problema kasi yung mga RD kinukuha, ginagawang Yusek, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:35:16.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004731)  
So, Mr. Speaker, in short, we're in good hands.

**[05:35:19.320] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004732)  
Yes, tingin ko po.

**[05:35:21.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004733)  
Tingin mo? Are we? Of course.

**[05:35:26.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004734)  
What are the respective qualifications?

**[05:35:29.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004735)  
Qualifications and prior experience in housing policy, urban planning, architecture, engineering,

**[05:35:36.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004736)  
housing finance, and real estate development or community development.

**[05:35:41.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004737)  
Kasi napaka-importante po kasi neto, no?

**[05:35:43.140] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004738)  
Siyempre, importante po, architecture, urban planning, and finance.

**[05:35:47.520] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004739)  
And ito si Secretary, si PA Engineer.

**[05:35:52.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004740)  
And you think, considering the scale and complexity of 4PH,

**[05:35:56.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004741)  
does the department believe that,

**[05:35:59.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004742)  
it currently has sufficient in-house technical expertise

**[05:36:03.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004743)  
to independently evaluate project feasibility, affordability, construction, estate management, and long-term...

**[05:36:11.260] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004744)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, with due diligence, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:36:14.020] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-004745)  
As mandated by the President po, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:36:18.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-004746)  
I have no more questions, Mr. Speaker.

**[06:33:03.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006032)

that the types,

**[07:09:53.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006633)  
that the types of, ah,

**[07:09:54.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006634)  
of housing units which are currently being constructed,

**[07:09:58.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006635)

**[07:10:00.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006636)

**[07:10:04.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006637)

**[07:10:06.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006638)  
But if we look at, ah,

**[07:10:11.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006639)

that land is exhaustible, hindi naman, ah,

**[07:10:21.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006641)  
na,

**[07:10:22.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006642)

**[07:10:28.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006643)

**[07:10:39.140] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006644)

**[07:10:44.540] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006645)  
Pero bakit babalik pa tayo sa ano?

**[07:10:48.520] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006646)  
Madam Speaker, there are areas po na, based tayo sa market driven, there are areas po na horizontal pa rin yung gusto nila.

**[07:10:57.320] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006647)  
Meron ding appropriate areas for horizontal, and meron appropriate areas for vertical, Madam Speaker.

**[07:11:04.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006648)  
Naalala ko dati sa Quezon City, nung unang, when I was still a city councilor, dahil maraming tao ang gusto ng horizontal,

**[07:11:17.420] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006649)  
yun yung focus nila.

**[07:11:18.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006650)  
Na ginawa nung, ng Quezon City government.

**[07:11:21.940] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006651)  
In fact, at the time, I was espousing for vertical construction.

**[07:11:26.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006652)  
Because the truth is, ang lupa, nauubos ang tao, hindi.

**[07:11:31.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006653)  
Ang lupa, tumataas yung presyo over time, ang tao dumarami.

**[07:11:38.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006654)  
And merong law of supply and demand.

**[07:11:40.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006655)  
While I understand na maraming tao sa probinsya, maaaring gusto yung horizontal,

**[07:11:48.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006656)  
but, di ba, minsan, we have to prepare for the eventuality, eh, na mauubos yung mga lupa natin,

**[07:11:58.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006657)  
at hindi natin mabibigyan ng sapat na pabahay ang mga nangangailangan ng bahay.

**[07:12:06.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006658)  
Yun yung, ah...

**[07:12:06.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006659)  
Madam Speaker, kaya po nag-fail yung una, kasi pinipilit nila yung vertical.

**[07:12:12.860] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006660)  
Kaya nagka-problema tayo in some areas, Madam Speaker.

**[07:12:17.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006661)  
Now,

**[07:12:18.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006662)  
look at, ewan ko kung doon tayo nag-fail. Later on, babalik ako dyan.

**[07:12:26.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006663)  
NHA performance.

**[07:12:28.540] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006664)  
Families actually provided housing. Target was 72.5%.

**[07:12:33.160] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006665)  
Actual na nabigyan, 53.5%.

**[07:12:36.480] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006666)  
NHA missed the target by 19 percentage points.

**[07:12:40.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006667)  
Vacant and unawarded housing. This is very telling.

**[07:12:43.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006668)  
Ah, 25% dapat ang target.

**[07:12:55.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006669)  
Pero, 84% remains unawarded.

**[07:13:00.980] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006670)  
Merong problema sa housing ang mga Pilipino.

**[07:13:04.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006671)  
Pero, 84% ng ready housing units, hindi naman mabigyan ng housing.

**[07:13:13.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006672)  
Nang binipisaryo.

**[07:13:16.300] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006673)  
In fact, babalik ako dyan sa, ano...

**[07:13:19.760] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006674)  
Ah...

**[07:13:21.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006675)  
In Rizal.

**[07:13:23.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006676)  
I remember during the 18th Congress,

**[07:13:27.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006677)

**[07:13:32.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006678)

**[07:13:36.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006679)  
mga tinayo sa Rizal, eh.

**[07:13:39.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006680)  
In fact, ang inabutan ko doon,

**[07:13:41.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006681)  
ewan ko kung ayalan total.

**[07:13:43.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006682)

**[07:13:46.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006683)  
Pero, pagpunta ko doon,

**[07:13:49.480] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006684)

**[07:13:52.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006685)

**[07:13:54.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006686)  
In fact, nasisira na yung mga ibang units.

**[07:13:57.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006687)  
Nasisira na.

**[07:13:58.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006688)  
Wala pa rin binipisaryo.

**[07:14:01.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006689)  
And, it's not only in Rizal.

**[07:14:04.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006690)  
It has happened in various other places.

**[07:14:11.320] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006691)  
Madam Speaker, sa totoo lang,

**[07:14:13.700] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006692)  
siya talaga tanggapin ng taga-Rizal,

**[07:14:15.380] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006693)

**[07:14:16.760] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006694)  
Pero, ngayon, sa 5,000 dati,

**[07:14:18.520] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006695)  
2,000 na lang nandun.

**[07:14:20.200] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006696)  
That was not the reason.

**[07:14:21.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006697)  
Because, at the time,

**[07:14:23.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006698)  
I was, ano, eh.

**[07:14:24.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006699)  
I was with Mayor Junjun Inariz, eh.

**[07:14:27.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006700)  
And, in fact, okay na sa kanya, eh.

**[07:14:30.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006701)  
Totoo, ang mga local government units,

**[07:14:32.420] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006702)  
especially the mayors,

**[07:14:33.580] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006703)

**[07:14:35.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006704)  
kasi madami rin mga

**[07:14:37.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006705)  
nangangailangan ng pabahay

**[07:14:40.660] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006706)  
sa aming lunsod.

**[07:14:42.220] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006707)  
So, gusto namin ang binipisaryo,

**[07:14:43.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006708)  
tiga sa amin.

**[07:14:45.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006709)  
But, the thing is,

**[07:14:46.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006710)  
they allow.

**[07:14:47.980] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006711)  
In fact, kaya nga,

**[07:14:48.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006712)  
meron tayong mga relocaties

**[07:14:50.380] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006713)  
na napupunta sa Bulacan,

**[07:14:53.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006714)  
meron sa Montalban.

**[07:14:55.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006715)  
And, assuming, assuming na

**[07:14:58.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006716)  
I would agree with the...

**[07:15:00.000] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006717)  
Madam Speaker, meron po ngayon provincial resolution that they will really not accept. In province lang, talaga accept na lang.

**[07:15:09.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006718)  
Kagaya ang sinabi mo, meron na ngayon. I'm talking about nung wala pa yung resolution na yun. And assuming na I would agree with what you said,

**[07:15:18.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006719)  
e di sana binigay na lang natin yung mga pabahay dun sa mga tigarizal, imbis na nasira yung mga housing units na iwan na unoccupied.

**[07:15:31.120] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006720)  
Madam Speaker, yun na po yung nangyari ngayon. Binigay na lang sa tigarizal.

**[07:15:43.620] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006721)  
The reason why I'm asking these questions.

**[07:15:48.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006722)  
Because we have to face actual facts. We should not sugarcoat it.

**[07:15:52.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006723)  
Kasi kung, if we do not recognize a problem, then we will not be able to come up with a solution.

**[07:15:58.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006724)  
Masasayang ang pera na, masasayang yung pabahay, yung oras na ginugol para gawin yung pabahay na yan.

**[07:16:06.660] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006725)  
Tapos hindi natin nababawasan yung mga taong nangangailangan ng tirahan.

**[07:16:11.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006726)  
Diba? That's my point, Madam Chair, our good sponsor.

**[07:16:17.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006727)  
Because if we do not provide a fallback to this, alam ko meron tayong mga guidelines na gustong gawin.

**[07:16:29.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006728)  
But as I mentioned, for practicality purposes, based on what is actually happening, if we just look at it really,

**[07:16:39.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006729)  
then makikita natin napakadaming pabahay na sasayang.

**[07:16:44.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006730)  
In fact, naalala ko, during the time of former president,

**[07:16:47.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006731)  
President Duterte, nakalimutan ko ano yung mga nag-occupy dun sa mga pabahay na hindi pa nabibigyan ng beneficiary.

**[07:16:56.100] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006732)  
That also became the problem.

**[07:16:59.740] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006733)  
Sino ba yun? Yung group that occupied yung mga housing units?

**[07:17:05.220] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006734)  
Maybe NHA would know, or they should.

**[07:17:08.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006735)  
That was an issue before.

**[07:17:11.120] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006736)  
Kadamay, Madam Speaker.

**[07:17:12.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006737)  
Kadamay, diba? Dahil anong sinabi nila, anong binigay nilang reason?

**[07:17:16.200] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006738)  
Sabi nila.

**[07:17:17.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006739)  
Kaya naman yung mga unit, ang tagal-tagal nang nakatiwangwang, ang tagal-tagal nang walang occupant,

**[07:17:22.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006740)  
eh, tutal, ayaw nyo naman ibigay sa beneficaryo, eh, o-occupy na namin.

**[07:17:29.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006741)  
Kaya, that was a problem before, and that remained a problem up to now.

**[07:17:36.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006742)  
So, ano yung ginawa natin?

**[07:17:39.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006743)  
What steps have we taken?

**[07:17:41.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006744)  
So that, as we construct units, we are able to...

**[07:17:47.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006745)  
put a family in this unit, para-housing units, para naman nagagamit na nila.

**[07:17:57.480] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006746)  
Madam Speaker, yun nga po.

**[07:17:59.700] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006747)  
Sa ngayon, from 5,000 units, 2,000 na lang un-unoccupied, at saka sa in-city na lang, in-province na lang yung beneficiary po ng NHA.

**[07:18:09.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006748)  
Now, the things that kailangan natin maayos.

**[07:18:17.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006749)  
As I mentioned, yung utilization, not only of the funds given to the SHUD,

**[07:18:24.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006750)  
but yung utilization ng actual housing units na readily available.

**[07:18:29.740] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006751)  
Pangalawa, there is also a problem with collection efficiency.

**[07:18:34.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006752)  
Target was 50% collection, but actual target was 27%.

**[07:18:38.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006753)  
This directly affects financial sustainability.

**[07:18:42.280] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006754)  
If NHA cannot collect efficiently revolving housing resources, will deteriorate.

**[07:18:47.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006755)  
Mas konti ang makaka-avail.

**[07:18:49.980] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006756)  
What improvements have we taken to improve collection efficiency?

**[07:18:56.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006757)  
Madam Speaker, kasi una, may mga moratorium pag may bagyo.

**[07:19:00.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006758)  
Especially ngayon na may problema tayo sa pagtaas ng crudo.

**[07:19:07.500] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006759)  
Kaya po yun yung problema, Madam Speaker.

**[07:19:09.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006760)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[07:19:11.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006761)  
Yung collection efficiency, yung tinatanong ko.

**[07:19:13.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006762)  
What steps?

**[07:19:17.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006763)  
What steps has the agency taken to improve collection efficiency?

**[07:19:26.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006764)  
Madam Speaker, meron na po ngayon silang online payment.

**[07:19:32.420] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006765)  
So, they improvised na po.

**[07:19:33.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006766)  
Yung online, dati kasi you have to sisigilin mo.

**[07:19:36.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006767)  
Ngayon may digitalization na sila.

**[07:19:38.620] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006768)  
Madam Speaker.

**[07:19:39.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006769)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[07:19:42.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006770)  
Madam Speaker.

**[07:19:45.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006771)  
Well, it's a well...

**[07:19:47.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006772)  
It's a welcome innovation that we agree with online payments.

**[07:19:51.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006773)  
But as we all know, the problem is not kung magbabayad ka ng physically o magbabayad ka ng online.

**[07:19:58.560] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006774)  
The problem really, it's really a collection problem.

**[07:20:02.020] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006775)  
Dahil meron talagang mga binipisaryo, ang kailangan mong pupuntahan para makulektahan mo ng pambayad eh.

**[07:20:11.160] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006776)  
They will not do it voluntarily.

**[07:20:17.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006777)  
Diba, sa atin na lang eh, hindi na bahay ah, sa cellphone.

**[07:20:22.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006778)  
Diba, dati pwede ka mag-accumulate ng utang sa cellphone bill mo.

**[07:20:26.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006779)  
Kita mo ngayon, ang ginagawa ng mga provider.

**[07:20:32.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006780)  
Puputulin yung linya mo para mapilitang kang magbayad.

**[07:20:38.560] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006781)  
So, NHA, ano bang kailangan nyo gawin?

**[07:20:41.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006782)  
Kasi hindi daw kayo na baka nag-i-evi, kaya hindi nagbabayad din yung mga owners.

**[07:20:47.520] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006783)  
But, you know, kailangan siguro ano, i-improve na lang yung collections.

**[07:20:53.560] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006784)  
Yeah, that's why, good sponsor.

**[07:20:54.940] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006785)  
And we'll need to make sure that intensify na lang ni NHA yung collections nila.

**[07:20:59.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006786)  
Actually, yun yung point ko, good sponsor, is what steps has the agency taken to improve yung collection efficiency

**[07:21:09.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006787)  
given its past experience of poor collection efficiency.

**[07:21:15.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006788)  
Dahil talagang mahirap maningil eh.

**[07:21:17.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006789)  
Ano yung point ko?

**[07:21:24.200] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006790)  
No.

**[07:21:34.420] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-006791)

**[07:21:38.280] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-006792)

**[07:56:38.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007359)  
So it's no longer an NHA project?

**[07:56:42.680] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007360)  
Yes, po.

**[07:56:43.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007361)  
Salamat po.

**[07:56:46.060] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007362)  
Hindi po ba itong malaking weakness in terms of policy?

**[07:56:53.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007363)  
Papapabayaan natin yung pinaghirapan natin na tayo po ay gumastos ng malaki para dito.

**[07:57:02.720] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007364)  
Nag-abono ang national government.

**[07:57:05.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007365)  
Pagkatapos po pagdito turnover, papabayaan na lang natin na mag-deteriorate ito sa mga local government.

**[07:57:13.080] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007366)  
Kaya po, Madam Speaker, yung current na plan po, yun nga, so rental na lang.

**[07:57:17.100] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007367)  
Kasi yung iba kasi, binibenta din nila.

**[07:57:19.640] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007368)  
Yun ang problema.

**[07:57:20.260] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007369)  
Kaya they're more into rental housing.

**[07:57:23.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007370)  
Tingnan lang si NHA.

**[07:57:25.480] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007371)  
Don't you think that there must be a certain degree of responsibility sa local governments?

**[07:57:34.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007372)  
Sinisiguro ng NHA na dapat nilang gawin kapag na-earn over nila dahil ito po ay proyekto naman ng national government.

**[07:57:46.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007373)  
At ang national government po ay nagbibigay naman ng allotment sa mga local government.

**[07:57:53.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007374)  
Pag-aralan po yan, Madam Speaker, ni NHA.

**[07:58:06.320] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007375)  
Sa policy po ng land use and zoning, ano po ba ang responsibilidad ng LISUD, especially for urban centers?

**[07:58:20.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007376)  
Madam Speaker, LISUD po.

**[07:58:23.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007377)  
Dito po ang nag-approve ng for highly-urbanized centers.

**[07:58:27.440] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007378)  
Dito po sa Metro Manila, how many NCRLGUs have current and approved comprehensive land use as of now?

**[07:58:38.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007379)  
There, 16.

**[07:58:41.920] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007380)  
Except Manila and Taguig, na-approve na.

**[07:58:46.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007381)  
So, 15.

**[07:58:48.080] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007382)  
16.

**[07:58:49.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007383)  
Except Manila and Taguig.

**[07:58:51.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007384)  
18 na yan, 17?

**[07:58:53.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007385)  
18 lang tayo eh.

**[07:58:54.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007386)  
Wala yung Pateros.

**[07:58:55.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007387)  
Sumobra kayo ng isa.

**[07:58:57.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007388)  
Pateros.

**[07:58:58.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007389)  
Ay, sinabi mo Pateros.

**[07:59:00.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007390)  
Without Pateros po.

**[07:59:02.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007391)  
15, municipality si Pateros.

**[07:59:05.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007392)  
Without Manila and?

**[07:59:07.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007393)  
Taguig.

**[07:59:08.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007394)  
Taguig.

**[07:59:10.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007395)  
Ay, anong Pateros? Meron.

**[07:59:15.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007396)  
Meron. Pero hindi municipality si Pateros kasi hindi siya…

**[07:59:19.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007397)  
Kung wala yung Taguig, wala yung Manila, ibig sabihin 15 lang.

**[07:59:23.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007398)  
Kasi Metro Manila is composed of 16 cities and 1 municipality. Tama.

**[07:59:29.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007399)  
So, 15, may dalawa na wala pa.

**[07:59:34.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007400)  
Ano pong nagiging problema? Bakit wala pa?

**[07:59:40.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007401)  
They are still updating their CLUP, Madam Speaker.

**[07:59:46.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007402)  
Pardon?

**[07:59:50.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007403)  
They are still updating.

**[07:59:51.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007404)  
May mga deadlines po ba yan?

**[07:59:57.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007405)  
Walang deadline but the earlier the better.

**[07:59:59.460] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007406)  
Madam Speaker, but they send them reminders naman. These hoods are giving them their reminders naman, Madam Speaker.

**[08:00:07.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007407)  
Ang ibig pong sabihin nun, kung wala pong zoning and land use,

**[08:00:15.920] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007408)  
paano po nag-ooperate na kapag bibigay ng zoning clearances itong mga LDUs na ito?

**[08:00:25.360] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007409)  
Meron naman po silang previews but hindi lang updated, Madam Speaker.

**[08:00:30.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007410)  
Ah, so based on the previews.

**[08:00:32.780] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007411)  
Previews, CLUP.

**[08:00:34.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007412)  
And the previews sa zoning ay approved din po?

**[08:00:39.380] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007413)  
Yes po, Madam Speaker.

**[08:00:40.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007414)  
So lahat approved. Hindi lang na-update.

**[08:00:43.860] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007415)  
Na-update.

**[08:00:44.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007416)  
Tama po. Sige po.

**[08:00:52.620] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007417)  
Ano sa tingin nyo in terms of sa Metro Manila Development Authority?

**[08:00:57.280] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007418)  
In terms of...

**[08:00:59.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007419)  
Ah...

**[08:00:59.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007420)  
Pagtulong para po sa programa ng housing, na ISF.

**[08:01:09.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007421)  
Don't you think that the MMDA is too weak?

**[08:01:17.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007422)  
Ah, we...

**[08:01:18.100] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007423)  
Ah, MD is not under po these hoods.

**[08:01:21.300] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007424)  
So I think we cannot commit on that, comment on that, and commit for them also, Madam Speaker.

**[08:01:27.880] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007425)  
But, again...

**[08:01:28.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007426)  
Because the Metro Manila is the only...

**[08:01:29.980] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007427)  
...coordinating body, it's under the office of the President, and of course, if you are in the...

**[08:01:40.100] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007428)  
Kung kayo po ay nagpa-plano ng urban development, or sa Metro...

**[08:01:43.480] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007429)  
Ah, wala pong relasyon ng DSUD at ang Metro Manila Development Authority in terms of urban planning sa Metro Manila?

**[08:01:50.900] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007430)  
Wala... Ano pong relasyon?

**[08:01:58.420] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007434)  
Madam

**[08:01:59.220] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007435)  
Speaker.

**[08:02:00.200] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007436)  
Sa Council nila, observer naman po si DSUD observer.

**[08:02:06.700] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007437)  
With the indulgence of our distinguished colleague, may we respectfully request him to please wind up.

**[08:02:14.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007438)  
But, of course, the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development is requested to coordinate with the Office of the Honorable Erice for all the nitty-gritty rules and regulations that he can

**[08:02:29.980] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007439)  
contribute, especially in terms of implementation.

**[08:02:32.550] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007444)  
Huli na lang po, Madam Speaker, ano po yung... Of course, meron po kayong proposal sa NEP at proposal sa DBCC, pero lumabas sa NEP, mas maliit po sa inyong proposal.

**[08:02:49.900] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007445)  
Ano po yung sa tingin nyo na pinakamahalaga na dapat na isama dito sa proposal na ito?

**[08:02:56.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007446)  
The Department, Madam Speaker, has already submitted.

**[08:02:59.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007447)  
Especially po sa CMP po, sa planning, SHAP-C, yun po Madam Speaker.

**[08:03:08.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007448)  
Ano po yung pinakamahalaga?

**[08:03:10.540] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007449)  
Doon sa na-

**[08:03:11.680] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007450)  
CMP po, siya SHAP-C po.

**[08:03:14.080] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007451)  
SHAP-C, Committee Mortgage Program, ito po.

**[08:03:22.720] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007452)  
Ang dami eh, ng priority.

**[08:03:25.340] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007453)  
Okay, can you just give me the most important ones?

**[08:03:28.340] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007454)  
Yes po, resubmit the priorities.

**[08:03:31.120] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007455)  
Naniniwala po ko na kailangan natin pag-igihan yung urban development, especially in urban centers.

**[08:03:38.720] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007456)  
Kasi po huling-huli na yung Metro Manila.

**[08:03:41.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007457)  
So, pwede po mabigyan niyo ako ng kopya at baka sakali na mapakiusapan natin na maisingit.

**[08:03:50.280] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007458)  
Pwede natin bawasan yung LGSF, dagdag natin sa D-SUD.

**[08:03:55.000] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007459)  
Salamat po.

**[08:03:55.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007460)  
Thank you so much Madam Speaker.

**[08:03:57.340] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007461)  
Thank you.

**[08:03:57.900] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007462)  
And maybe the Honorable Erice can also make the proper motion.

**[08:04:04.320] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-007463)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we terminate the discussion on the Department of Human Settlement and Urban Development.

**[08:04:15.920] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007464)  
Majority Leader.

**[08:04:17.300] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-007465)  
Madam Speaker, on the part of the majority, we join the minority in the motion to terminate the period of interpellation

**[08:04:23.280] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-007466)  
on the proposed budget of the Department of Human Settlements.

**[08:04:27.900] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-007467)  
And urban development, including attached agency and corporation.

**[08:04:32.620] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007468)  
An objection?

**[08:04:34.140] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007469)  
Heeding then the discussion on the interpellation of the proposed budget of the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development,

**[08:04:42.480] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007470)  
including its attached agencies, agency and corporations, is hereby terminated.

**[08:04:48.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007471)  
Thank you so much Madam Speaker.

**[08:04:50.020] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007472)  
May this budget translate into more homes, stronger communities, and better future for people.

**[08:04:56.080] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-007473)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[08:04:57.900] Rep. Katrina Reiko Chua-Tai** (asr-007474)  
Madam Speaker, I move to suspend session for a few minutes.

**[08:15:49.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007498)  
Session resumed. Majority Leader.

**[08:15:53.760] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007498-split-2)

Asek Aurelio Beltran

Asek Quintin Magsico Jr.

**[08:17:04.100] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007510)  
Asek Atty. Marjorie Aizon

**[08:17:06.620] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007511)  
Asek Atty. Facundo Palafox

**[08:17:10.220] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007512)  
Asek Atty. Virgilio Mendez

**[08:17:13.840] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007513)  
Asek Arsenio Ponce

**[08:17:16.540] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007514)  
Asek Rene Calocar

**[08:17:19.300] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007515)  
And all present are the directors and staff of the Agrarian Reform.

**[08:17:26.900] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007516)  
Sa ating mga masisipag,

**[08:17:30.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007517)  
magigiting at walang pagod na mga kawaninang department

**[08:17:33.620] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007518)  
of Agrarian Reform headed by one of our idol

**[08:17:38.120] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007519)  
and the pillars of the previous Congresses,

**[08:17:41.120] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007520)  
Secretary Conrado Estrella III,

**[08:17:44.040] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007521)  
maraming salamat po sa inyong pagdalo.

**[08:17:46.400] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007522)  
Majority Leader?

**[08:17:48.060] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007523)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable

**[08:17:51.980] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007524)  
Ian Paul Dy of the 3rd District of Isabela,

**[08:17:56.140] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007525)  
Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations,

**[08:18:00.000] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007526)  
to sponsor the proposed budget of the Department of Agrarian Reform.

**[08:18:04.020] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007527)  
The Honorable Ian Paul Dy,

**[08:18:07.040] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007528)  
Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations,

**[08:18:09.060] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007529)  
is hereby recognized to sponsor the proposed budget

**[08:18:11.980] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007530)  
of the Department of Agrarian Reform.

**[08:18:14.540] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007531)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[08:18:18.040] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007532)  
Esteemed colleagues in this August Chamber,

**[08:18:21.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007533)  
I rise today to sponsor the proposed budget

**[08:18:24.480] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007534)  
of the Department of Agrarian Reform

**[08:18:26.620] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007535)  
for fiscal year 2027.

**[08:18:30.000] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007536)  
A proposed allocation of 16,935,368,000

**[08:18:36.180] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007537)  
that represents far more than fiscal allocation.

**[08:18:40.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007538)  
It represents lives transformed,

**[08:18:43.320] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007539)  
historical promises kept,

**[08:18:45.580] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007540)  
and a national commitment to social justice

**[08:18:48.040] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007541)  
that is now entering its decisive final phase.

**[08:18:52.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007542)  
Agrarian Reform is not merely a government administrative program.

**[08:18:56.620] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007543)  
At its heart, it is an instrument of restorative justice,

**[08:19:00.000] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007544)  
and it is our collective duty to correct

**[08:19:02.080] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007545)  
deep historical inequities,

**[08:19:06.180] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007546)  
and empower the very hands that feed our nation,

**[08:19:09.040] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007547)  
our farmers.

**[08:19:10.580] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007548)  
When we secure land ownership,

**[08:19:13.180] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007549)  
when we lift decades of suffocating debt,

**[08:19:16.680] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007550)  
when we construct infrastructure to help our farmers,

**[08:19:20.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007551)  
and when we deliver free legal protection,

**[08:19:23.680] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007552)  
we are not simply funding economic activities,

**[08:19:27.680] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007553)  
we are investing in human dignity,

**[08:19:29.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007554)  
food security,

**[08:19:31.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007555)  
and rural emancipation under President Ferdinand R. Marcos Jr.'s vision

**[08:19:36.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007556)  
of a Bagong Pilipinas.

**[08:19:38.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007557)  
Allow me to ground this sponsorship in concrete reality.

**[08:19:42.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007558)  
Under the Marcos Jr. administration,

**[08:19:45.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007559)  
from July 2022 through August 12, 2026,

**[08:19:50.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007560)  
DAR has achieved an unprecedented cumulative distribution

**[08:19:54.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007561)  
of 410,179 land titles,

**[08:20:03.400] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007564)  
covering 556,088 hectares,

**[08:20:06.700] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007565)  
and benefiting 376,738 agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[08:20:11.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007566)  
This reflects an extraordinary trajectory

**[08:20:14.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007567)  
of operational acceleration.

**[08:20:18.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007568)  
In fiscal year 2023,

**[08:20:21.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007569)  
69,897 titles were distributed,

**[08:20:25.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007570)  
covering 84,044 hectares

**[08:20:28.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007571)  
for 73,399 ARBs.

**[08:20:33.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007572)  
In fiscal year 2024,

**[08:20:35.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007573)  
102,536 titles were distributed,

**[08:20:40.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007574)  
covering 120,772 hectares

**[08:20:44.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007575)  
for 88,310 ARBs.

**[08:20:50.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007576)

to agrarian reform

**[08:24:06.940] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007677)  
Beneficiaries.

In agrarian reform

**[08:25:05.960] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007698)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Representative Kaka Bag-ao

**[08:25:12.860] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007699)  
of the Lone District of Dinagat Island

**[08:25:14.920] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-007700)  
for her interpellation of the proposed budget of the Department of Agrarian Reform.

**[08:25:18.820] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-007701)  
The Honorable Kaka Bag-ao is recognized. Please proceed.

**[08:25:22.600] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007702)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[08:25:24.580] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007703)  
At magandang gabi po.

**[08:25:26.520] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007704)  
Magandang gabi din po.

**[08:25:28.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007706)  
At saka happy birthday, Secretary Estrella.

**[08:25:33.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007707)  
Sa araw ng ilong karanasan talaga, ay kaarawan, ay trabaho din po kayo.

**[08:25:39.140] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007708)  
So, ikilan ko na lang pa para makapag-birthday pa po kayo, sir.

**[08:25:44.400] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007709)  
Yung isang tinong ko, Mr. Speaker,

**[08:25:47.220] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007710)  
alam po natin lahat.

**[08:25:48.820] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007711)  
At ito po ay tanong din ng maraming magkasaka.

**[08:25:51.720] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007712)  
Ay nung August 11 dialogue ng maraming mga CSOs at ng DAR

**[08:25:56.500] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007713)  
ay iba-iba po ang naging sagot ng mga opisyal ng DAR

**[08:26:00.080] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007714)  
tungkol sa DOJ Opinion No. 16 Series of 2026.

**[08:26:06.180] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007715)  
Dalawa po yung malaging pwedeng maging implikasyon niya.

**[08:26:09.400] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007716)  
Una yung medyo klarong statement noon ng DOJ

**[08:26:13.180] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007717)  
na wala nang Agrarian Reform, wala nang DAR.

**[08:26:15.700] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007718)  
Para kaya ang tanong ko ay bakit pa tayo nandito ngayong gabi

**[08:26:18.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007719)  
kung wala na po wala kayo.

**[08:26:20.300] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007720)  
Yung pangalawa po implikasyon yan ay yun pong

**[08:26:22.900] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007721)  
yung polisiya tungkol dun sa pagkoprotekta ng 5-hectare retention limit.

**[08:26:29.940] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007722)  
So, at marami po ang pwedeng mag-implikasyon yan.

**[08:26:33.740] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007723)  
Kasama po yan yung pagtatanong kung paano nga iseset yung remaining targets

**[08:26:39.660] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007724)  
for distribution of land.

**[08:26:41.900] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007725)  
Kasi hindi naman din natin sinasabi na wala na tayong natitirang lands for distribution.

**[08:26:46.880] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007726)  
Siguro po ang tanong ko,

**[08:26:48.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007727)  
una at pangkalahatan sa bahaging ito ay

**[08:26:52.420] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007728)  
ano po yung klarong posisyon ng Department, Mr. Sponsor,

**[08:26:57.580] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007729)  
doon sa DOJ Opinion No. 16?

**[08:27:01.380] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007730)  
At ano po ang plano ng DAR para harapin po yung opinion na yan?

**[08:27:15.260] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007731)  
Okay, ma'am. Maraming salamat po.

**[08:27:18.260] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007732)  
So, your question was for the DOJ's...

**[08:27:24.060] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007733)  
Ano pong posisyon ng DAR? Kasi tahimik po ang DAR.

**[08:27:26.500] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007734)  
Iba-iba po ang opinion ng DAR tungkol dyan.

**[08:27:28.480] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007735)  
DOJ Opinion po ito. Opinion ng Department of Justice.

**[08:27:32.560] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007736)  
Ano pong tingin ng DAR opinion tungkol sa opinion na yan?

**[08:27:36.300] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007737)  
Okay, this is not...

**[08:27:43.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007738)  
Mahalaga lang po ang tanong dahil may kinalaman din po ito sa...

**[08:27:48.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007739)  
Una, sa buhay mismo ng agrarian reform, no?

**[08:27:51.000] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007740)  
Kung mahalaga pa bang pinag-uusapan natin ang Departamento,

**[08:27:54.680] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007741)  
kung wala na po ba siya, talagang tinatapos na po ba ng DOJ

**[08:27:57.960] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007742)  
ang buhay ng agrarian reform at ng DAR

**[08:28:00.900] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007743)  
at ang kanyang mga gusto pang i-target na i-accomplish

**[08:28:04.520] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007744)  
para sa agrarian reform program.

**[08:28:07.200] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007745)  
Okay, ma'am.

**[08:28:08.480] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007746)  
So, for that, ano po, the salient features of that...

**[08:28:12.260] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007747)  
of this removal was the economic recovery priority.

**[08:28:18.260] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007748)  
And the formally raised during the economic recovery meeting

**[08:28:22.000] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007749)  
as well as the big bold reform.

**[08:28:37.760] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007750)  
So, yes, we're merely following the law that the DOJ has set

**[08:28:41.920] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007751)  
for the removal of the 5-hectare retention limit under the CARP.

**[08:28:45.820] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007752)  
Actually, hindi po po siya law, Mr. Sponsor.

**[08:28:47.940] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007753)  
Ito po.

**[08:28:48.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007754)  
So, ang tanong ko po ay ano po ang posisyon ng DAR sa opinion ng DOJ

**[08:28:55.660] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007755)  
kung isang implikasyon nito ay nangunguhulugan,

**[08:28:59.360] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007756)  
ay maaaring mga hulugan na wala na ang agrarian reform

**[08:29:03.580] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007757)  
kasi yun po ang sinasabi ng opinion.

**[08:29:06.340] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007758)  
Yes, well, clearly, we still have land reform.

**[08:29:52.000] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007766)  
So, yes, ma'am.

**[08:29:53.700] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007767)  
So, there, we are reading here, it's the implements the DOJ legal opinion.

**[08:30:00.000] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007768)  
No. 16-2026, dated March 9, 2026, confirming that retention limits under Republic Act No. 6657 are no longer applicable.

**[08:30:12.060] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007769)  
And the request for Land Registration Authority Administrator Gerardo Pangas Sirios requested a Department of Justice opinion on Feb. 18, 2026.

**[08:30:23.300] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007770)  
The issue: whether Department of Agrarian Reform land transfer clearance remains necessary for registering private agricultural land transfers after the June 30, 2014 cutoff under Republic Act No. 6657, as amended.

**[08:30:40.820] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007771)  
The DOJ's ruling: the acquisition and distribution period under Republic Act No. 6657 was already extended by Republic Acts No. 8532 and

**[08:30:53.300] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007772)  
9700 and deemed June 30, 2014 as the final cutoff.

**[08:31:00.160] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007773)  
The proceedings initiated before the cutoff may continue to finality.

**[08:31:05.840] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007774)  
Consequently, the general provisions of Republic Act No. 6657, including the 5-hectare retention limit,

**[08:31:14.120] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007775)  
inapplicable to new transactions after June 30, 2014, consistent with the doctrine that when the reason for a law ceases,

**[08:31:22.300] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007776)  
the law itself.

**[08:31:23.300] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007777)  
Implications, the requirement for a Department of Agrarian Reform land transfer clearance and under Administrative Order No. 04 no longer has legal basis supporting its renewal.

**[08:31:39.860] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007778)  
And we streamlined the process, removed that requirement for a land transfer clearance under DAR-AO No. 04, easing administrative hurdles.

**[08:31:51.280] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007779)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Sponsor.

**[08:31:53.300] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007780)  
Dal birthday ngayon ni Secretary Estella, iisipin ko na lang po na hindi pa po kayo gano'ng kaseryoso sa inyong sagot.

**[08:32:03.340] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007781)  
Ang kahilingan ko na lang, Sec, siguro sa DAR, dahil matagal ko na po kayo kasama, taniniwala po ako sa inyo.

**[08:32:11.160] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007782)  
In fact, sinusuportahan ko pa talaga yung budget niyo.

**[08:32:14.260] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007783)  
Sana lang po ay, kung pwede, mula ngayon po hanggang sa panahon na kailangan na nating magbalik ulit sa lamesa at mag-usap,

**[08:32:23.300] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007784)  
ay balikan po natin yung sagot na yan.

**[08:32:27.560] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007785)  
Tingin ko hindi po natatapos sa agrarian reform, kaso yun po ang implication ng DOJ opinion.

**[08:32:33.600] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007786)  
I'm sure a lot of people in the DAR are also as confused as a lot of farmers asking for clarification for this question.

**[08:32:43.340] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007787)  
So, iasaan ko po na open-ended pa po yung sagot ni Mr. Sponsor at naipalawanag naman niyang mabuti ang sinasabi ng laman.

**[08:32:53.300] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007788)  
Iaasahan ko lang po ay, pwede po bang mas maging bukas pa tayo sa mas marami pang konsultasyon,

**[08:32:58.720] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007789)  
lalo na sa pagkaklaro, kasi ang automatic pong interpretasyon ng lahat,

**[08:33:04.040] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007790)  
kahit po nung nasa dinagat ako, itinatawagan ako ng mga magkasaka,

**[08:33:07.700] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007791)  
ang sabi sa akin ay, wala ng CARP, wala ng DAR, so hindi na kayo mag-budget briefing.

**[08:33:13.560] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007792)  
So, marami pong confusion, Mr. Sponsor.

**[08:33:16.740] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007793)  
So, I'm sure it will be very helpful for the DAR not to be very conclusive about,

**[08:33:23.300] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007794)  
it says, in the opinion, but be open to a lot of consultations.

**[08:33:27.980] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007795)

**[08:41:27.820] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007912)

**[08:41:36.140] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007914)

**[08:41:45.560] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007915)  
the estimated amount and collections have been going down

**[08:41:49.440] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007916)

**[08:41:51.280] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007917)

**[08:42:00.760] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007918)

**[08:42:04.120] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007919)

**[08:42:06.660] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007920)  
2025, 28 million.

**[08:42:08.460] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007921)  
Now, we're down to 10.295,710.59.

**[08:42:14.080] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007922)  
So...

**[08:42:15.740] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007923)  
Yeah.

**[08:42:16.860] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007924)

**[08:43:16.740] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-007939)

**[08:43:19.160] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007941)

**[08:45:17.020] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007963)  
At medyo nahuhuli po tayo. Meron po tayong kakatapos lang debate natin kanina tungkol sa urban development. Baka ho pa na ho na para i-transition din natin ng DAR papunta sa isang Department of Rural Development framework na reach to reef ang concept.

**[08:45:36.820] Rep. Arlene "Kaka" J. Bag-ao** (asr-007964)

**[09:21:28.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008936)  
Chair, thank you. While waiting for the slides, let me just explain. Kakabigay lang po kasi ng data, so medyo delayed po yung pag-transmit. Let me be clear: ngayon lang po naibigay ng DAR yung data na hiningi natin, at nakabatay doon yung mga slides. Kaya

**[09:21:58.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008937)  
ito po ang dahilan ng delay. Anyway, habang hinihintay po natin na maihabol, may mga preliminary questions po tayo. My questions have to do with landowner compensation. In the 2026 GAA, there is an appropriation of 500 million for landowner compensation. Sa 2027 naman, 100 million.

**[09:22:28.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008938)  
There's a proposal for 100 million for landowner compensation. Okay. Before we go into the details, yung ilang mga basic concepts po muna para malinaw sa ating discussion. Dito po sa information na binigay ninyo, nakalagay 'list of unbooked approved claims with memorandum of valuation,' MOV, for landowner compensation.

**[09:22:58.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008939)  
And then, of course, there's a listing of claims, land area, total valuation, and estimated cash portion. For purposes of our discussion, siguro ipaliwanag lang natin yung ilang mga terms. For example, yung memorandum of valuation, ano po ibig sabihin noon? Sabi kasi dito,

**[09:23:28.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008940)  
'list of unbooked approved claims with memorandum of valuation,' MOV.

**[09:23:37.200] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-008940-split-2)  
It means it's already been valued by Land Bank to be paid to the landowners.

**[09:23:43.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008940-split-3)  
Okay, so parang dokumento po na iyon na may specific assessment doon sa parcel of land na iyon?

**[09:23:51.500] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-008940-split-4)  
Yes. Yes po.

**[09:23:52.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008940-split-5)  
Okay, hindi lang siya estimate based on

**[09:23:58.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008941)  
general rates. Tiningnan yung lupa at ito ang value nito, ganoon?

**[09:24:08.700] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-008941-split-2)
